📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期基孔肯雅病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • QHTD组在所有主要终点指标上均表现出统计学上的显著优势:第3天总体治疗反应率显著提高,第3天和第7天中医证候评分平均下降幅度更大(所有P < 0.05),发热、关节痛和皮疹的缓解率更高且缓解时间更短,3个月随访时不良反应和关节痛后遗症的发病率更低。
  • 我们的高分辨率研究识别了与埃及伊蚊数量和基孔肯雅热传播风险相关的微尺度脆弱性。 [10.1371/journal.pntd.0014776]。
  • 纯化的CHIKV颗粒在所有储存条件下均表现出70.4%的回收率,并且病毒颗粒形态得以保留。然而,感染力随时间逐渐下降,且在所测试的方案中未观察到显著差异。 [10.21203/rs.3.rs-11183026/v1]。
  • 17只猕猴(42.5%)检测到中和抗体,其中16只(40.0%)检测到针对DENV的中和抗体,1只(2.5%)检测到针对CHIKV的中和抗体。检测到针对DENV-1、DENV-2和DENV-3的中和抗体,但未检测到针对DENV-4、ZIKV和JEV的抗体。所有样本中均未发现病毒RNA。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • The QHTD group exhibited statistically superior outcomes across all primary endpoints: significantly higher overall treatment response rate on Day 3, greater mean reductions in TCM syndrome scores on Days 3 and 7 (all P < 0.05), faster resolution rates and shorter resolution times for fever, arthralgia, and rash, and lower incidence of adverse reactions and arthralgia sequelae at the 3-month follow-up.
  • Our high-resolution study identified fine-scale vulnerabilities linked to both Aedes aegypti abundance and heightened risk of CHIKV transmission. [10.1371/journal.pntd.0014776].
  • Purified CHIKV particles showed a 70.4% recovery rate and preserved virion morphology across all storage conditions.
  • Seventeen macaques (42.5%) had neutralizing antibodies, with 16 (40.0%) showing antibodies against DENV and one (2.5%) against CHIKV.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期基孔肯雅病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 一项高分辨率研究通过分析蚊虫繁殖模式和人类血清阳性率,绘制了巴西贫民窟中基孔肯雅病毒的风险地图,揭示了社会经济和环境因素如何影响传播。 [news-dbf23d2f657f1a60]。
  • 由廖宇华及其同事发表在《PLOS Pathogens》上的一项新研究分析了三种东/中/南非(ECSA)基因型基孔肯雅病毒亚谱系的分子进化,揭示了针对不同蚊媒媒介的适应性策略。 [news-aa0ce7def68004c3]。
  • 文章讨论了由于依赖临床病例检测,东非地区检测基孔肯雅热暴发的挑战,这会导致延迟。它指出基孔肯雅热传播模式已从森林型向城市型转变,涉及埃及伊蚊。 [news-d8f5e1ba25b5a0c8]。
  • 香港卫生防护中心(CHP)在持续的蚊虫繁殖季期间警告登革热(DF)和基孔肯雅热(CF)的风险。CHP记录显示,2026年10月2日至10月9日期间发现一例输入性登革热病例和一例输入性基孔肯雅热病例。该基孔肯雅热患者在潜伏期内曾前往中国大陆,并在旅行期间感染。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Chikungunya virus news developments.
  • A high-resolution study mapped chikungunya virus risk in a Brazilian slum by analyzing mosquito breeding patterns and human seropositivity, revealing how socioeconomic and environmental factors influence transmission.
  • A new study published in PLOS Pathogens by Yuhua Liao and colleagues analyzed the molecular evolution of three sub-lineages of the East/Central/South African (ECSA) genotype of Chikungunya virus, revealing distinct adaptive strategies for different mosquito vectors.
  • The article discusses the challenges of detecting chikungunya outbreaks in East Africa due to reliance on clinical case detection, which introduces delays.
  • The Centre for Health Protection (CHP) warned of dengue fever (DF) and chikungunya fever (CF) risks during the ongoing mosquito breeding season.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

纳米孔测序用于基孔肯雅病毒:原理与应用。

Nanopore Sequencing for Chikungunya Virus: Principles and Application.
作者： Adelino TÉR, Xavier J, Fonseca V, Giovanetti M
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨纳米孔测序在基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)基因组学中的应用,以应对在疫情期间快速、便携和准确追踪病毒多样性及传播的迫切需求。
设计与对象
本研究聚焦于纳米孔测序在基孔肯雅病毒中的原理与应用,考察其在临床和环境样本中用于恢复完整病毒基因组和监测病毒传播的使用情况。
核心方法
纳米孔测序通过测量核酸穿过纳米级孔时的离子电流变化,直接对RNA和DNA进行测序,从而实现对基孔肯雅病毒基因组的实时、单分子分析,无需依赖培养。
主要结果
该方法展示了从临床和环境样本中快速恢复完整病毒基因组的能力,有助于在疫情期间实时追踪病毒多样性、传播及扩散。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为病原体监测领域的重要进展,特别是在资源有限的环境中,纳米孔测序提供了一种灵活且强大的工具,用于基孔肯雅病毒的监测。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为基孔肯雅病毒的更有效监测策略的制定提供依据,从而增强疫情响应和疫情应对能力。
局限与证据强度
本研究承认在碱基调用和错误校正方面存在挑战,但强调了稳健的质量控制流程,确保了可靠的结果,支持了中等程度的证据可信度。

Nanopore Sequencing for Chikungunya Virus: Principles and Application.

Authors: Adelino TÉR, Xavier J, Fonseca V, Giovanetti M
Original abstract (English)
Nanopore sequencing is transforming viral genomics through real-time, portable, long-read analysis of RNA and DNA. Unlike traditional short-read platforms, it detects nucleotide sequences by measuring ionic current changes as nucleic acids pass through nanoscale pores, enabling direct single-molecule sequencing and base modification detection. Its simplicity, flexibility, and capacity for ultra-long reads make it ideal for resolving complex genomic regions, structural variants, and full viral genomes. These advantages have accelerated its use in pathogen surveillance and outbreak response, especially in resource-limited settings. For chikungunya virus (CHIKV), nanopore sequencing allows rapid, culture-independent recovery of complete genomes from clinical and vector samples, enabling real-time tracking of viral diversity, evolution, and spread. Experiences from Ebola, Zika, and COVID-19 have demonstrated the power of portable sequencing, now applied to CHIKV monitoring. Advances in tools such as Guppy, Dorado, Minimap2, and Medaka enhance read quality, consensus accuracy, and downstream analyses. Despite challenges in basecalling and error correction, robust quality control pipelines ensure reliable results. Ongoing improvements in chemistry, flow cell design, and machine learning will further enhance fidelity and throughput, establishing nanopore sequencing as a cornerstone of CHIKV genomic surveillance and epidemic preparedness.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the application of nanopore sequencing in Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genomics, addressing the need for rapid, portable, and accurate methods to track viral diversity and evolution during outbreaks.
Design and population
The study focused on the principles and application of nanopore sequencing for Chikungunya virus, examining its use in clinical and vector samples to recover complete genomes and monitor viral spread.
Core methods
Nanopore sequencing was used to directly sequence RNA and DNA by measuring ionic current changes as nucleic acids pass through nanoscale pores, enabling real-time, single-molecule analysis of Chikungunya virus genomes without the need for culture.
Main results
The method demonstrated the ability to rapidly recover full viral genomes from clinical and vector samples, facilitating real-time tracking of viral diversity, evolution, and spread during outbreaks.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as a significant advancement in pathogen surveillance, particularly for resource-limited settings, where nanopore sequencing provides a flexible and powerful tool for monitoring Chikungunya virus.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more effective genomic surveillance strategies for Chikungunya virus, enhancing outbreak response and epidemic preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges challenges in basecalling and error correction but highlights robust quality control pipelines that ensure reliable results, supporting a moderate confidence in the evidence.

基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)概述、生物学及系统发育

Overview of Chikungunya Virus Epidemiology, Biology, and Pathogenesis.
作者： Castro EF, Filomatori C
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV),一种属于Togaviridae科的虫媒α病毒,主要通过埃及伊蚊(Aedes aegypti)和白纹伊蚊(Aedes albopictus)传播。该病毒引起一种急性发热性疾病,特征为严重的、常为双侧性关节痛,可能进展为慢性肌肉骨骼疼痛,并偶有涉及心血管和神经系统系统的全身并发症。CHIKV具有球形、包膜的病毒颗粒(约70 nm),呈T = 4二十面体对称结构,包含介导受体结合和膜融合的E1/E2糖蛋白异二聚体。其正链RNA基因组(约11.8 kb)编码用于复制的非结构蛋白和用于病毒颗粒组装的结构蛋白。病毒复制发生在细胞质囊泡中,涉及基因组和亚基因组RNA的合成,随后是糖蛋白成熟和在细胞膜上出芽。从流行病学角度看,CHIKV已扩展至非洲和亚洲以外的地区,其主要传播模式由适应性进化增强其传播能力。自2013年引入美洲以来,全球发病率仍保持较高水平,2025年报告了超过180,000例确诊病例。预防策略依赖于媒介控制和疫苗接种;基于病毒样颗粒(VLP)的疫苗(如Vimkunya)显示出前景,而减毒活疫苗则面临安全性问题。目前尚无获批的抗病毒药物;现有管理措施为支持性治疗,但针对病毒复制和免疫调节的试验性疗法正在研发中。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV),一种属于Togaviridae科的虫媒α病毒,主要通过埃及伊蚊(Aedes aegypti)和白纹伊蚊(Aedes albopictus)传播。该病毒引起一种急性发热性疾病,特征为严重的、常为双侧性关节痛,可能进展为慢性肌肉骨骼疼痛,并偶有涉及心血管和神经系统系统的全身并发症。
设计与对象
本研究概述了基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)的结构、生物学及系统发育。研究内容包括病毒的结构、复制机制及其全球传播情况。分析涵盖了CHIKV谱系及其相关疾病。
核心方法
基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)具有球形、包膜的病毒颗粒(约70 nm),呈T = 4二十面体对称结构,包含介导受体结合和膜融合的E1/E2糖蛋白异二聚体。其正链RNA基因组(约11.8 kb)编码用于复制的非结构蛋白和用于病毒颗粒组装的结构蛋白。病毒复制发生在细胞质囊泡中,涉及基因组和亚基因组RNA的合成,随后是糖蛋白成熟和在细胞膜上出芽。
主要结果
自2013年引入美洲以来,全球发病率仍保持较高水平,2025年报告了超过180,000例确诊病例。
解释与创新
从流行病学角度看,CHIKV已扩展至非洲和亚洲以外的地区,其主要传播模式由适应性进化增强其传播能力。预防策略依赖于媒介控制和疫苗接种;基于病毒样颗粒(VLP)的疫苗(如Vimkunya)显示出前景,而减毒活疫苗则面临安全性问题。
科研与公卫意义
目前尚无获批的抗病毒药物;现有管理措施为支持性治疗,但针对病毒复制和免疫调节的试验性疗法正在研发中。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告局限性或证据强度。

Overview of Chikungunya Virus Epidemiology, Biology, and Pathogenesis.

Authors: Castro EF, Filomatori C
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), an arthropod-borne alphavirus within the Togaviridae family, is transmitted primarily by Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus. The virus causes an acute febrile illness characterized by severe, often bilateral polyarthralgia, with potential progression to chronic musculoskeletal pain and rare systemic complications involving cardiovascular and neurological systems. CHIKV exhibits a spherical, enveloped virion (~70 nm) with T = 4 icosahedral symmetry, incorporating E1/E2 glycoprotein heterodimers that mediate receptor binding and membrane fusion. Its positive-sense RNA genome (~11.8 kb) encodes nonstructural proteins for replication and structural proteins for virion assembly. Replication occurs in cytoplasmic spherules, involving synthesis of genomic and subgenomic RNAs, followed by glycoprotein maturation and budding at the plasma membrane. Epidemiologically, CHIKV has expanded beyond Africa and Asia, with major outbreaks driven by adaptive mutations enhancing transmission via A. Since introduction to the Americas in 2013, the global incidence remains high, with >180,000 confirmed cases reported in 2025. Preventive strategies rely on vector control and vaccination; VLP-based vaccines (e.g., Vimkunya) show promise, while live-attenuated formulations face safety concerns. No licensed antivirals exist; current management is supportive, though investigational therapies targeting viral replication and immune modulation are under development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), an arthropod-borne alphavirus within the Togaviridae family, is transmitted primarily by Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus. The virus causes an acute febrile illness characterized by severe, often bilateral polyarthralgia, with potential progression to chronic musculoskeletal pain and rare systemic complications involving cardiovascular and neurological systems.
Design and population
The study provides an overview of Chikungunya virus epidemiology, biology, and pathogenesis. It includes information on the virus's structure, replication mechanisms, and its global spread. The analysis covers CHIKV lineages and their associated diseases.
Core methods
CHIKV exhibits a spherical, enveloped virion (~70 nm) with T = 4 icosahedral symmetry, incorporating E1/E2 glycoprotein heterodimers that mediate receptor binding and membrane fusion. Its positive-sense RNA genome (~11.8 kb) encodes nonstructural proteins for replication and structural proteins for virion assembly. Replication occurs in cytoplasmic spherules, involving synthesis of genomic and subgenomic RNAs, followed by glycoprotein maturation and budding at the plasma membrane.
Main results
Since introduction to the Americas in 2013, the global incidence remains high, with >180,000 confirmed cases reported in 2025.
Interpretation and novelty
Epidemiologically, CHIKV has expanded beyond Africa and Asia, with major outbreaks driven by adaptive mutations enhancing transmission via A. Preventive strategies rely on vector control and vaccination; VLP-based vaccines (e.g., Vimkunya) show promise, while live-attenuated formulations face safety concerns.
Scientific and public-health significance
No licensed antivirals exist; current management is supportive, though investigational therapies targeting viral replication and immune modulation are under development.
Limitations and evidence strength

Journal: Tropical Medicine and Health  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-08  |  DOI: 10.1186/s41182-026-01089-0  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 54, 1

马来西亚半岛城市—森林交界处猕猴中登革病毒和基孔肯雅病毒暴露的中和抗体证据

Neutralizing antibody evidence of dengue and chikungunya virus exposure in macaques from an urban–forest fringe in Peninsular Malaysia
作者： Jecelyn Leaslie John, Mya Myat Ngwe Tun, Merveille Kapandji, Hani Kartini Agustar, Janeeca Sam, Sasie Kala S. Kanasan, Jaeyres Jani, Shingo Inoue, Takeshi Urano, Kouichi Morita
摘要中文翻译登革病毒(DENV)、基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)、寨卡病毒(ZIKV)和日本脑炎病毒(JEV)等虫媒病毒在蚊虫媒介和脊椎动物宿主之间循环传播,而非人灵长类动物(NHPs)在丛林型传播循环中起着重要作用。在马来西亚,长尾猕猴(Macaca fascicularis)与人类在马来西亚国家大学(UKM)班金分校校园内的频繁互动,为研究人类—野生动物界面的虫媒病毒暴露提供了机会。从UKM班金分校校园内的五个地点采集了40只自由活动的猕猴血清样本。使用90%焦点减少中和试验(FRNT 90)检测血清样本中针对DENV、CHIKV、ZIKV和JEV的中和抗体,同时用RT–qPCR检测全血中的病毒RNA。17只猕猴(42.5%)检测到中和抗体,表明其曾暴露于虫媒病毒。具体而言,16只(40.0%)检测到针对DENV的中和抗体,1只(2.5%)检测到针对CHIKV的中和抗体。检测到针对DENV-1、DENV-2和DENV-3的中和抗体,但未检测到针对DENV-4、ZIKV和JEV的抗体。所有样本中均未发现病毒RNA。据我们所知,这是马来西亚首次使用FRNT 90中和抗体检测方法研究自由活动猕猴的虫媒病毒暴露。这些发现为马来西亚猕猴在人类—野生动物界面的DENV和CHIKV暴露提供了新的证据,并强调了对猕猴、蚊虫媒介和人类进行整合的纵向监测研究的必要性,以更好地理解虫媒病毒生态学和传播动态。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨人类—野生动物界面的虫媒病毒暴露情况,特别是关注非人灵长类动物在登革病毒和基孔肯雅病毒等虫媒病毒丛林型传播循环中的作用。
设计与对象
从马来西亚国家大学(UKM)班金分校校园内的五个地点采集了40只自由活动的猕猴样本,该校园是猕猴与人类频繁互动的区域。
核心方法
使用90%焦点减少中和试验(FRNT 90)检测血清样本中针对登革病毒(DENV)、基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)、寨卡病毒(ZIKV)和日本脑炎病毒(JEV)的中和抗体,同时用RT–qPCR检测全血中的病毒RNA。
主要结果
17只猕猴(42.5%)检测到中和抗体,其中16只(40.0%)检测到针对DENV的中和抗体,1只(2.5%)检测到针对CHIKV的中和抗体。检测到针对DENV-1、DENV-2和DENV-3的中和抗体,但未检测到针对DENV-4、ZIKV和JEV的抗体。所有样本中均未发现病毒RNA。
解释与创新
本研究首次在马来西亚使用FRNT 90中和抗体检测方法,提供了自由活动猕猴暴露于虫媒病毒的证据,突显了这些动物在DENV和CHIKV虫媒病毒传播动态中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现有助于理解虫媒病毒在人类—野生动物界面的生态学和传播动态,强调了对猕猴、蚊虫媒介和人类进行整合监测的必要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于横断面设计和缺乏纵向数据,这可能影响研究结果的普遍适用性。由于基于抗体检测而非直接病毒分离,证据强度中等。

Neutralizing antibody evidence of dengue and chikungunya virus exposure in macaques from an urban–forest fringe in Peninsular Malaysia

Authors: Jecelyn Leaslie John, Mya Myat Ngwe Tun, Merveille Kapandji, Hani Kartini Agustar, Janeeca Sam, Sasie Kala S. Kanasan, Jaeyres Jani, Shingo Inoue, Takeshi Urano, Kouichi Morita
Original abstract (English)
Arboviruses such as dengue virus (DENV), chikungunya virus (CHIKV), Zika virus (ZIKV), and Japanese encephalitis virus (JEV) circulate between mosquito vectors and vertebrate hosts, with non-human primates (NHPs) playing an important role in sylvatic transmission cycles. In Malaysia, frequent interactions between long-tailed macaques (Macaca fascicularis) and humans at the Universiti Kebangsaan Malaysia (UKM) Bangi campus provide an opportunity to investigate arbovirus exposure at the human–wildlife interface. Forty free-ranging macaques were sampled from five locations within the UKM Bangi campus. Serum samples were screened for neutralizing antibodies against DENV, CHIKV, ZIKV, and JEV using the 90% focus reduction neutralization test (FRNT 90 ), while whole blood was tested for viral RNA by RT–qPCR. Seventeen macaques (42.5%) had neutralizing antibodies, indicating previous arbovirus exposure. Specifically, 16 (40.0%) had neutralizing antibodies against DENV and one (2.5%) against CHIKV. Neutralizing antibodies against DENV-1, DENV-2, and DENV-3 were detected, whereas DENV-4, ZIKV, and JEV antibodies were not detected. No viral RNA was identified in any sample. To our knowledge, this is the first study in Malaysia to investigate arbovirus exposure in free-ranging macaques using FRNT 90 neutralizing antibody assays. These findings provide updated evidence of DENV and CHIKV exposure in macaques at the human–wildlife interface and highlight the need for integrated longitudinal surveillance of NHPs, mosquito vectors, and humans to better understand arbovirus ecology and transmission dynamics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate arbovirus exposure at the human–wildlife interface, particularly focusing on the role of non-human primates in sylvatic transmission cycles of viruses like dengue and chikungunya.
Design and population
Forty free-ranging macaques were sampled from five locations within the Universiti Kebangsaan Malaysia (UKM) Bangi campus, where frequent interactions between macaques and humans occur.
Core methods
Serum samples were screened for neutralizing antibodies against dengue virus (DENV), chikungunya virus (CHIKV), Zika virus (ZIKV), and Japanese encephalitis virus (JEV) using the 90% focus reduction neutralization test (FRNT 90), while whole blood was tested for viral RNA by RT–qPCR.
Main results
Seventeen macaques (42.5%) had neutralizing antibodies, with 16 (40.0%) showing antibodies against DENV and one (2.5%) against CHIKV. Neutralizing antibodies against DENV-1, DENV-2, and DENV-3 were detected, whereas DENV-4, ZIKV, and JEV antibodies were not detected. No viral RNA was identified in any sample.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides the first evidence in Malaysia of arbovirus exposure in free-ranging macaques using FRNT 90 neutralizing antibody assays, highlighting the importance of these animals in the transmission dynamics of DENV and CHIKV.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings contribute to understanding the ecology and transmission dynamics of arboviruses at the human–wildlife interface, emphasizing the need for integrated surveillance of macaques, mosquito vectors, and humans.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and the absence of longitudinal data, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence is moderate as it is based on antibody detection rather than direct viral isolation.

利用维甲酸衍生物三氧化二砷对抗基孔肯雅病毒感染

Repurposing tretinoin, a vitamin A derivative, against Chikungunya virus infection
作者： Soumyajit Ghosh, Chandan Mahish, Archana Mahapatra, Tathagata Mukherjee, Pratima Kumari, Bijita Bhowmick, Saikat De, Udvas Ghorai, Rajshree Rajmohan Jena, Seema Pradhan
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)作为一种全球性病原体重新出现,对公共卫生构成严重威胁,尤其是由于缺乏特定的抗病毒药物。由于缺乏针对CHIKV的特定抗病毒药物,促使广泛研究以寻找治疗性疗法。
设计与对象
本研究利用体外、体内和离体模型评估了三氧化二砷(TR)或全反式视黄酸(ATRA)的抗CHIKV活性。TR在CHIKV感染的病毒进入阶段和早期阶段表现出显著的抗病毒活性和有效抑制作用。TR的抗CHIKV潜力在小鼠模型和初级人类骨骼肌成肌细胞中进行了评估,表明其具有强大的临床前疗效。
核心方法
视黄醇类,维生素A衍生物,已被证明在对抗多种病毒感染方面具有抗病毒药物潜力,但其对甲型病毒的疗效尚未被研究。本研究中,TR在Vero和C2C12细胞中高效地抑制了CHIKV感染,显著降低了病毒RNA、蛋白质和子代病毒的形成(>90%)。全球转录组分析显示,CHIKV感染后“视黄酸信号传导”通路显著富集,视黄酸受体(RARs),特别是RAR-β,是TR抗CHIKV作用的关键介导因子。
主要结果
TR对CHIKV的IC50估计为Vero细胞中20.05 μM,C2C12细胞中19.71 μM。此外,TR通过减少病毒载量保护C57BL/6小鼠免受CHIKV挑战,从而降低了临床评分并提高了存活率。
解释与创新
本研究首次展示了TR对抗CHIKV感染的再利用潜力,鼓励其在未来的治疗药物中进行临床验证。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,TR可能是开发针对CHIKV的抗病毒药物治疗药物的有前景候选者,这对于缺乏特定治疗的CHIKV来说至关重要。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告本研究的局限性。

Repurposing tretinoin, a vitamin A derivative, against Chikungunya virus infection

Authors: Soumyajit Ghosh, Chandan Mahish, Archana Mahapatra, Tathagata Mukherjee, Pratima Kumari, Bijita Bhowmick, Saikat De, Udvas Ghorai, Rajshree Rajmohan Jena, Seema Pradhan
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) has reemerged as a global pathogen causing serious public health threat, particularly due to the absence of specific antivirals. Retinoids, vitamin A derivatives, have demonstrated antiviral potential against different viral infections, yet their efficacy against alphaviruses has never been investigated. In this study, tretinoin (TR), or all-trans retinoic acid (ATRA), was evaluated for its anti-CHIKV activity using in vitro , in vivo , and ex vivo models. TR abrogated CHIKV infection efficiently in Vero and C2C12 cells, with a marked reduction (>90%) in viral RNA, proteins, and progeny formation. The IC 50 of TR against CHIKV was estimated to be 20.05 μM and 19.71 μM in Vero and C2C12 cells, respectively. Additionally, TR showed remarkable virucidal activity and effective inhibition during entry, as well as in the early stages of post-CHIKV infection. Next, the global transcriptome profile demonstrated that the “signaling by retinoic acid” pathway was significantly enriched after CHIKV infection, and retinoic acid receptors (RARs), specifically RAR-β, are the key mediators for the anti-CHIKV effect of TR. Further, the drug alters phosphorylated mitogen-activated protein kinases (MAPKs), NF-κB, and proinflammatory cytokines during CHIKV infection in RAW 264.7 cells. Interestingly, TR protected C57BL/6 mice from CHIKV challenge by diminishing viral burden, leading to reduced clinical scores and better survival. Similar observations were also noticed in the ex vivo models of CHIKV-infected primary human skeletal muscle myoblasts (HSMMs). In conclusion, this work demonstrated the repurposing potential of TR against CHIKV infection for the first time, encouraging its clinical validation toward future therapeutics. IMPORTANCE Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) is an arbovirus causing Chikungunya fever (CHIKF), leading to debilitating arthralgia and myalgia. The unavailability of licensed antivirals against CHIKV encourages extensive research to find a therapeutic cure. Retinoids, the derivatives of vitamin A, have been repurposed as antiviral agents against a broad range of viruses; however, their potency against alphaviruses remains unexplored. Accordingly, tretinoin (TR), also known as all-trans retinoic acid (ATRA), was validated for anti-CHIKV efficacy in this investigation. Significant perturbations of CHIKV titer, RNA, and protein levels were observed in the presence of TR following infection, and its antiviral effect was mediated through RAR-β. Moreover, the anti-CHIKV potential of TR was evaluated in the mouse model and primary human skeletal muscle myoblasts, indicating strong preclinical efficacy. Finally, this is the first study to report the antiviral property of TR against CHIKV infection using in vitro , in vivo , and ex vivo approaches, suggesting its repurposing potential.

从野外采集的蚊虫中检测和测序基孔肯雅病毒的方案。

Protocol for Detecting and Sequencing Chikungunya Virus from Field-Collected Mosquitoes.
作者： Banho CA, Nascimento OB, Sacchetto L, Vasilakis N, Nogueira ML
摘要中文翻译蚊媒疾病是公共卫生领域的重要挑战,尤其是在热带地区,环境条件可能有利于蚊虫媒介的繁殖和传播。因此,对蚊虫种群中基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)进行早期和准确的检测,可以成为有效监测流行变异株和识别新病毒引入的宝贵工具。鉴于在野外捕获的蚊虫中检测蚊媒病毒的挑战,我们描述了一种集成的工作流程,用于CHIKV的分子检测和全基因组测序。该方案包括使用基于珠子的机械破碎仪对蚊虫进行匀浆,使用TRIzol试剂进行RNA提取(略有修改),使用CHIKV特异性RT-qPCR进行分子筛选,以及全基因组扩增后在Illumina平台上进行测序。尽管该方案针对单个蚊虫进行了优化,但仍能获得高质量的RNA,适用于虫媒监测和基因组分析。由于该方案能够从蚊虫样本中回收完整的CHIKV 基因组,因此可以作为基因组流行病学研究的基础,实现对病毒多样性及谱系动态的监测,并有助于早期发现新兴变异株,从而支持在流行和高风险地区及时、有针对性的公共卫生干预措施。

由于该方案能够从蚊虫样本中恢复完整的CHIKV 基因组,它可作为基因组流行病学研究的基础,实现病毒多样性及谱系动态的监测,并有助于早期发现新兴变种,从而支持在流行区和高风险地区及时、有针对性的公共卫生干预措施。

鉴于在野外捕获的蚊虫中检测虫媒病毒的困难,我们描述了一种整合的工作流程,用于CHIKV的分子检测和全基因组测序。
核心方法
该方案包括使用珠子基机械破碎仪对蚊虫进行匀浆,使用TRIzol试剂进行RNA提取(略有修改),使用CHIKV特异性RT-qPCR进行分子筛选,以及全基因组扩增后在Illumina平台上进行测序。

Protocol for Detecting and Sequencing Chikungunya Virus from Field-Collected Mosquitoes.

Authors: Banho CA, Nascimento OB, Sacchetto L, Vasilakis N, Nogueira ML
Original abstract (English)
Arboviral diseases represent a major public health challenge, especially in tropical regions where environmental conditions may favor the proliferation and spread of mosquito vectors. Thus, early and accurate detection of chikungunya virus (CHIKV) in mosquito populations can be a valuable tool for effective surveillance of circulating variants and for identifying new viral introductions. Given the challenges of detecting arboviruses in field-captured mosquitoes, we describe an integrated workflow for CHIKV molecular detection and whole-genome sequencing. This protocol includes mosquito homogenization using a bead-based mechanical disruptor, RNA extraction using TRIzol reagent with minor modifications, molecular screening using CHIKV-specific RT-qPCR, and whole-genome amplification followed by sequencing on Illumina platforms. Despite the protocol being optimized for individual mosquitoes, it results in high-quality RNA suitable for both entomological surveillance and genomic analysis. As this protocol allows recovery of complete CHIKV genomes from mosquito specimens, it can serve as a basis for genomic epidemiology studies, enabling monitoring of viral diversity and lineage dynamics, and facilitating early detection of emerging variants to support timely and targeted public health interventions in endemic and at-risk regions.

Given the challenges of detecting arboviruses in field-captured mosquitoes, we describe an integrated workflow for CHIKV molecular detection and whole-genome sequencing.
Core methods
This protocol includes mosquito homogenization using a bead-based mechanical disruptor, RNA extraction using TRIzol reagent with minor modifications, molecular screening using CHIKV-specific RT-qPCR, and whole-genome amplification followed by sequencing on Illumina platforms.

全球遗传多样性及进化在基孔肯雅病毒 E2糖蛋白中的模式

Global patterns of genetic diversity and evolution in chikungunya virus E2 glycoprotein
作者： Nouh Saad Mohamed, Jean Claude Semuto Ngabonziza, Ayman Ahmed
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)中E2糖蛋白的遗传多样性及进化,重点关注其在宿主细胞附着、免疫识别和谱系分化中的作用。
设计与对象
本研究分析了来自不同地区、宿主和流行病学时期的序列数据集,以研究CHIKV E2糖蛋白的遗传结构。利用Fst进行的种群结构分析表明,不同地区的种群之间存在显著的遗传分化,反映了基因流动受限和局部化的进化轨迹。
核心方法
本研究使用来自不同地区、宿主和流行病学时期的序列数据集,对E2的遗传多样性进行了全面分析。为了评估地理、宿主和时间对病毒种群结构的影响,进行了时空分层分析,利用固定指数检验(Fst)进行评估。
主要结果
核苷酸多样性在不同时期存在显著差异,其中扩张前时期(2004年前)表现出最高的多样性(Pi = 0.085),而近期的暴发时期则显示出相对较低的多样性(Pi = 0.012)。中性检验显示,在三个CHIKV E2谱系中均存在正选择和纯化选择的信号,支持了基于密码子的分析结果,即在整个E2基因中普遍存在纯化约束,但在谱系特异性位点则出现了反复的多样化信号。
解释与创新
这些发现突显了CHIKV E2糖蛋白的时空进化特性,揭示了可能影响免疫逃逸和流行病学动态的谱系特异性适应性变化。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为理解塑造CHIKV E2糖蛋白的进化压力提供了见解,可能有助于制定监测策略、疫苗开发以及了解病毒适应性和免疫逃逸机制。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其依赖于序列数据而没有直接的实验验证,且由于缺乏详细的统计控制和序列采样中的潜在偏差,证据强度中等。

Global patterns of genetic diversity and evolution in chikungunya virus E2 glycoprotein

Authors: Nouh Saad Mohamed, Jean Claude Semuto Ngabonziza, Ayman Ahmed
Original abstract (English)
The E2 glycoprotein of chikungunya virus (CHIKV) mediates host-cell attachment and entry and contains key antigenic determinants targeted by host immune responses. Variation in E2 can influence host interaction, immune recognition, lineage diversification, and epidemic fitness, it represents a biologically relevant target for assessing evolutionary pressures and epidemic dynamics. In this study, we conducted a comprehensive analysis of E2 genetic diversity using a dataset of sequences collected across regions, hosts, and epidemic periods. Diversity indices, neutrality tests, and haplotypes network analysis were applied to characterize evolutionary pressures acting on E2 glycoprotein. To assess the influence of geography, host, and time on viral population structure, we performed spatial–temporal stratification and analysis to assess clustering patterns across lineages and regions using Fixation index test (Fst). Nucleotide diversity varied considerably across time, with the pre-expansion period (pre-2004) showing the highest diversity (Pi = 0.085), while more recent outbreak periods exhibited comparatively reduced diversity (Pi = 0.012). Neutrality tests revealed signatures of both purifying and positive selection across the three CKIKV E2 lineages (negatively statistically significant Tajima D, Fu and Li’s D, and Fu and Li’s F tests, p -values < 0.05) supported by codon‐based analyses showing pervasive purifying constraint across the E2 gene but recurrent diversifying signals at lineage‐specific sites, notably codon 146 in the West African lineage, codons 220, 245, 247, and 254 in the Asian lineage, and codons 54, 56, 102, 145, 163, 177, and 350 in the East/Central/South/African lineage. Population structure analyses using Fst indicated significant genetic differentiation between regional populations, reflecting restricted gene flow and localized evolutionary trajectories. Haplotype network analysis highlighted distinct clustering patterns shaped by geography and epidemic waves, reflecting the persistence of lineage-specific signatures across regions. At the same time, reduced connectivity among recent outbreak strains point to more discrete intercontinental movement events, likely driven by human-mediated vector dispersal or travel-associated introductions. Collectively, these findings underscore the spatiotemporal evolution of CHIKV E2 glycoprotein and its implications for viral adaptation and immune evasion.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the genetic diversity and evolution of the E2 glycoprotein in chikungunya virus (CHIKV), focusing on its role in host-cell attachment, immune recognition, and lineage diversification.
Design and population
The study analyzed a dataset of sequences collected across different regions, hosts, and epidemic periods to examine the genetic structure of CHIKV E2 glycoprotein. Population structure analyses using Fst indicated significant genetic differentiation between regional populations, reflecting restricted gene flow and localized evolutionary trajectories.
Core methods
The study conducted a comprehensive analysis of E2 genetic diversity using a dataset of sequences collected across regions, hosts, and epidemic periods. To assess the influence of geography, host, and time on viral population structure, spatial–temporal stratification and analysis were performed using Fixation index test (Fst).
Main results
Nucleotide diversity varied considerably across time, with the pre-expansion period (pre-2004) showing the highest diversity (Pi = 0.085), while more recent outbreak periods exhibited comparatively reduced diversity (Pi = 0.012). Neutrality tests revealed signatures of both purifying and positive selection across the three CHIKV E2 lineages, supported by codon‐based analyses showing pervasive purifying constraint across the E2 gene but recurrent diversifying signals at lineage‐specific sites.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the spatiotemporal evolution of CHIKV E2 glycoprotein, revealing lineage-specific adaptive changes that may influence immune evasion and epidemic dynamics.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the evolutionary pressures shaping CHIKV E2 glycoprotein, which could inform surveillance strategies, vaccine development, and understanding of viral adaptation and immune evasion mechanisms.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on sequence data without direct experimental validation, and the evidence is moderate due to the absence of detailed statistical controls and potential biases in sequence sampling.

使用牛津纳米孔技术的 基孔肯雅病毒 长读长扩增子测序工作流程。

Workflow for Long-Read Amplicon Sequencing of Chikungunya Virus Using Oxford Nanopore Technology.
作者： Branda F, Ciccozzi M, Scarpa F
摘要中文翻译该协议使用针对 Oxford Nanopore Technologies (ONT) 平台优化的基于扩增子的策略,为 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 的全 基因组 测序提供全面的分步工作流程。该程序包括样品处理、病毒 RNA 提取、质量控制、cDNA 合成、多重 PCR 扩增、文库制备、测序和初级生物信息处理的详细说明。该协议旨在最大限度地提高实验室的重现性,适用于 基因组监测 应用,包括 暴发 调查和分子 流行病学,即使在处理低至中等病毒载量时也是如此。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在使用 Oxford Nanopore Technologies (ONT) 平台开发可靠的 基因组 (CHIKV) 全 基因组 测序工作流程,满足处理 基因组监测 中低至中等病毒载量的改进方法的需求。
设计与对象
该研究描述了样品处理、病毒 RNA 提取、质量控制、cDNA 合成、多重 PCR 扩增、文库制备、测序和初级生物信息处理的分步方案,但没有提及特定人群或样本量。
核心方法
该程序包括样品处理、病毒 RNA 提取、质量控制、cDNA 合成、多重 PCR 扩增、文库制备、测序和初级生物信息处理的详细说明。
主要结果
该协议的开发是为了最大限度地提高实验室的重现性,适用于 基因组监测 应用,包括 暴发 调查和分子 流行病学,即使在处理低至中等病毒载量时也是如此。
解释与创新
作者将该协议解释为一种新颖且可重复的 CHIKV 基因组测序方法,强调其在 监测 和流行病学研究中的实用性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为基因组监测为基孔肯雅病毒所做的努力提供信息,通过提供可靠的测序工作流程来支持暴发研究和分子流行病学。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的具体局限性,但该方案的设计具有可重复性,并且适合低至中等病毒载量,表明证据强度适中。

Workflow for Long-Read Amplicon Sequencing of Chikungunya Virus Using Oxford Nanopore Technology.

Authors: Branda F, Ciccozzi M, Scarpa F
Original abstract (English)
This protocol provides a comprehensive, step-by-step workflow for whole-genome sequencing of Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) using an amplicon-based strategy optimized for Oxford Nanopore Technologies (ONT) platforms. The procedure includes detailed instructions for sample handling, viral RNA extraction, quality control, cDNA synthesis, multiplex PCR amplification, library preparation, sequencing, and primary bioinformatic processing. The protocol is designed to maximize reproducibility across laboratories and is suitable for genomic surveillance applications, including outbreak investigation and molecular epidemiology, even when working with low-to-moderate viral loads.

基孔肯雅病毒 nsP2蛋白的C端部分与宿主蛋白MK2和MK3相互作用,促进高效感染

The C-terminal part of the Chikungunya virus nsP2 protein interacts with host proteins MK2 and MK3 to facilitate efficient infection
作者： Supriya Suman Keshry, Ankita Datey, Bijita Bhowmick, Prabhudutta Mamidi, Bharat Bhusan Subudhi, Soma Chattopadhyay
摘要中文翻译宿主病毒蛋白相互作用在驱动病毒感染和发病机制中起着关键作用。MK2和MK3是p38 MAPK家族的丝氨酸/苏氨酸蛋白激酶,已被证实与基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)感染相关。本研究旨在通过计算机模拟和体外方法阐明MK2/MK3与病毒蛋白nsP2之间的相互作用及其在CHIKV感染中的意义。本研究显示,在CHIKV感染过程中,nsP2与宿主激酶MK2和MK3发生相互作用。值得注意的是,nsP2的C端(CT)结构域(nsP2-CT;氨基酸[aa] 422-798)参与了这一相互作用。此外,研究还发现nsP2-CT仅在CHIKV感染存在的情况下与MK2相互作用,而MK3则可以在没有CHIKV感染的情况下与nsP2-CT相互作用。另外,nsP2-CT的两个亚片段CT1(aa 458-621)和CT2(aa 603-798)也被发现与MK2相互作用。相比之下,MK3并未与任何nsP2-CT片段发生相互作用。这些相互作用的重要性通过在MK2或MK3过表达的情况下评估病毒感染(有或无nsP2-CT)得到了进一步验证。研究结果表明,MK2或MK3的过表达和抑制会通过降低病毒滴度来损害病毒感染;然而,当MK2与nsP2-CT共转染时,这种抑制作用被逆转。总之,本研究证实,在CHIKV感染过程中,nsP2-CT与宿主蛋白MK2和MK3发生相互作用,这些相互作用似乎有助于高效的CHIKV感染,这将有助于识别控制CHIKV感染的潜在治疗靶点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宿主病毒蛋白相互作用在驱动病毒感染和发病机制中起着关键作用。本研究旨在探讨基孔肯雅病毒 nsP2蛋白与宿主激酶MK2和MK3之间的相互作用及其意义。
设计与对象
本研究采用计算机模拟和体外方法来研究基孔肯雅病毒 nsP2蛋白与宿主蛋白MK2和MK3之间的相互作用。研究评估了在MK2或MK3过表达的情况下,有或无nsP2-CT时的病毒感染情况。
核心方法
本研究使用计算机模拟和体外方法来研究基孔肯雅病毒 nsP2蛋白与宿主激酶MK2和MK3之间的相互作用。
主要结果
本研究显示,在基孔肯雅病毒感染过程中,nsP2与宿主激酶MK2和MK3发生相互作用。此外,研究还发现nsP2-CT仅在CHIKV感染存在的情况下与MK2相互作用,而MK3则可以在没有CHIKV感染的情况下与nsP2-CT相互作用。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,nsP2-CT与MK2或MK3之间的相互作用对于高效的基孔肯雅病毒感染至关重要,突显了其作为控制感染潜在治疗靶点的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些相互作用可能为针对MK2或MK3的治疗策略提供信息,以控制基孔肯雅病毒感染。研究结果还提供了关于CHIKV 发病机制分子机制的见解。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于计算机模拟和体外方法,可能无法完全反映体内条件。证据强度中等,因为研究结果基于实验相互作用及其对病毒滴度的影响。

The C-terminal part of the Chikungunya virus nsP2 protein interacts with host proteins MK2 and MK3 to facilitate efficient infection

Authors: Supriya Suman Keshry, Ankita Datey, Bijita Bhowmick, Prabhudutta Mamidi, Bharat Bhusan Subudhi, Soma Chattopadhyay
Original abstract (English)
Host-viral protein interactions play a crucial role in driving viral infection and pathogenesis. MK2 and MK3, serine/threonine protein kinases of the p38 MAPK family, have been implicated in Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) infection. This investigation aimed to elucidate the interaction between MK2/MK3 and the viral protein nsP2 and to explore its significance in CHIKV infection through in silico and in vitro approaches. This study demonstrated that nsP2 interacts with host kinases MK2 and MK3 during CHIKV infection. Notably, the C-terminal (CT) domain of nsP2 (nsP2-CT; amino acids [aa] 422-798) was found to be involved in this interaction. Furthermore, it was revealed that nsP2-CT interacts with MK2 only in the presence of CHIKV infection, while MK3 exhibits this interaction independently of CHIKV infection. Additionally, two sub-fragments of nsP2-CT, CT1 (aa 458-621) and CT2 (aa 603-798), were also found to interact with MK2. In contrast, MK3 did not interact with any of the nsP2-CT fragments. The importance of these interactions was further validated by assessing viral infection in the presence of MK2 or MK3 overexpression, with or without nsP2-CT. The findings suggest that overexpression and inhibition of MK2 or MK3 impair viral infection by reducing viral titers; however, the inhibition was rescued by cotransfection of MK2 with nsP2-CT. In conclusion, this study confirms that nsP2-CT interacts with host proteins MK2 and MK3 during CHIKV infection, and that these interactions appear to contribute to efficient CHIKV infection, which will help identify potential therapeutic targets to control CHIKV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Host-viral protein interactions play a crucial role in driving viral infection and pathogenesis. This study aimed to explore the significance of interactions between the Chikungunya virus nsP2 protein and host kinases MK2 and MK3.
Design and population
This investigation employed in silico and in vitro approaches to study the interaction between the Chikungunya virus nsP2 protein and host proteins MK2 and MK3. The study assessed viral infection in the presence of MK2 or MK3 overexpression, with or without nsP2-CT.
Core methods
The study used in silico and in vitro methods to investigate the interaction between the Chikungunya virus nsP2 protein and host kinases MK2 and MK3.
Main results
This study demonstrated that nsP2 interacts with host kinases MK2 and MK3 during Chikungunya virus infection. Furthermore, it was revealed that nsP2-CT interacts with MK2 only in the presence of CHIKV infection, while MK3 exhibits this interaction independently of CHIKV infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the interaction between nsP2-CT and MK2 or MK3 is critical for efficient Chikungunya virus infection, highlighting a potential therapeutic target for controlling the infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These interactions may inform the development of therapeutic strategies targeting MK2 or MK3 to control Chikungunya virus infection. The results also provide insights into the molecular mechanisms underlying CHIKV pathogenesis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in silico and in vitro approaches, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions. The evidence is moderate, as the findings are based on experimental interactions and their effects on viral titers.

清热化湿透疹汤治疗基孔肯雅热的临床疗效及机制研究

Clinical Efficacy and Mechanistic Investigation of Qingre Huashi Touzhen Decoction in Treating Chikungunya Fever
作者： Zhuang Jieqin, Ma Qixin, Fan Qiyan, Nie Hao, Ye Ye
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 基孔肯雅热(CF)是一种由蚊媒传播的病毒性疾病,可引起急性发热性疾病和致残性慢性关节痛。清热化湿透疹汤(QHTD)是CF指南推荐的一线传统中药(TCM)治疗方案,亟需进一步研究其疗效及潜在机制。方法 进行了一项多中心回顾性队列研究研究。根据严格的纳入和排除标准,收集了2025年8月至10月期间诊断为急性期 ShiYu HuaRe 综合征(SYHRS)的CF患者的临床数据。最初筛选了135例QHTD组患者(常规支持治疗+QHTD)和255例对照组患者(仅常规支持治疗)。经过1:1倾向评分匹配后,每组最终纳入130例患者。主要结局指标包括总体治疗反应率、第3天和第7天的中医证候评分、主要症状(发热、关节痛和皮疹)的缓解率和缓解时间、不良反应的发病率以及3个月随访时关节痛后遗症的发病率。机制验证采用网络药理学、分子对接和动力学方法,量化QHTD的成分、靶点、通路、结合亲和力和稳定性。结果 QHTD组在所有主要终点指标上均表现出统计学上的显著优势:第3天总体治疗反应率显著提高,第3天和第7天中医证候评分平均下降幅度更大(所有P < 0.05),发热、关节痛和皮疹的缓解率更高且缓解时间更短,3个月随访时不良反应和关节痛后遗症的发病率更低。解释与创新性 作者将研究结果解释为QHTD在改善CF患者临床结局方面的有效性。本研究的创新性在于其全面评估了QHTD在治疗CF中的疗效和机制。科学与公共卫生意义 研究结果可以谨慎地支持将QHTD作为CF潜在辅助治疗方案的使用,特别是在提高治疗反应率和减少症状持续时间及后遗症方面。局限性与证据强度 本研究是一项回顾性队列研究研究,可能引入偏倚和混杂因素。由于研究设计和数据收集与分析的潜在局限性,证据强度中等。

科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以谨慎地支持将QHTD作为CF潜在辅助治疗方案的使用,特别是在提高治疗反应率和减少症状持续时间及后遗症方面。

Clinical Efficacy and Mechanistic Investigation of Qingre Huashi Touzhen Decoction in Treating Chikungunya Fever

Authors: Zhuang Jieqin, Ma Qixin, Fan Qiyan, Nie Hao, Ye Ye
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Chikungunya fever (CF) is a mosquito-borne viral disease causing acute febrile illness and debilitating chronic arthralgia. Qingre Huashi Touzhen Decoction (QHTD), the guideline-preferred first-line Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) regimen for CF, urgently warrants further study into its efficacy and underlying mechanisms. Methods A multicenter retrospective cohort study was conducted. Clinical data of CF patients diagnosed with acute-phase ShiYu HuaRe Syndrome (SYHRS) from August to October 2025 were collected according to strict inclusion and exclusion criteria. A total of 135 patients in the QHTD group (conventional supportive therapy + QHTD) and 255 patients in the control group (conventional supportive therapy alone) were initially screened. Following 1:1 propensity score matching, 130 patients were finally included in each group. Outcome measures included overall treatment response rate and TCM syndrome scores on Days 3 and 7, resolution rates and resolution times of primary symptoms (fever, arthralgia, and rash), incidence of adverse reactions, and the incidence of arthralgia sequelae at the 3-month follow-up. Mechanistic validation employed network pharmacology, molecular docking, and dynamics to quantify QHTD's components, targets, pathways, binding affinity, and stability. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chikungunya fever (CF) is a mosquito-borne viral disease causing acute febrile illness and debilitating chronic arthralgia. Qingre Huashi Touzhen Decoction (QHTD), the guideline-preferred first-line Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) regimen for CF, urgently warrants further study into its efficacy and underlying mechanisms.
Design and population
A multicenter retrospective cohort study was conducted. Clinical data of CF patients diagnosed with acute-phase ShiYu HuaRe Syndrome (SYHRS) from August to October 2025 were collected according to strict inclusion and exclusion criteria. A total of 135 patients in the QHTD group (conventional supportive therapy + QHTD) and 255 patients in the control group (conventional supportive therapy alone) were initially screened.
Core methods
Methods included a multicenter retrospective cohort study. Following 1:1 propensity score matching, 130 patients were finally included in each group. Outcome measures included overall treatment response rate and TCM syndrome scores on Days 3 and 7, resolution rates and resolution times of primary symptoms (fever, arthralgia, and rash), incidence of adverse reactions, and the incidence of arthralgia sequelae at the 3-month follow-up.
Main results
The QHTD group exhibited statistically superior outcomes across all primary endpoints: significantly higher overall treatment response rate on Day 3, greater mean reductions in TCM syndrome scores on Days 3 and 7 (all P < 0.05), faster resolution rates and shorter resolution times for fever, arthralgia, and rash, and lower incidence of adverse reactions and arthralgia sequelae at the 3-month follow-up.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence of QHTD's effectiveness in improving clinical outcomes for CF patients. The study's novelty lies in its comprehensive evaluation of QHTD's efficacy and mechanisms in treating CF.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can cautiously inform the use of QHTD as a potential adjunct therapy for CF, particularly in improving treatment response rates and reducing symptom duration and sequelae.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a retrospective cohort study, which may introduce bias and confounding factors. The evidence strength is moderate due to the study design and potential limitations in data collection and analysis.

针对基孔肯雅病毒 nsP2pro的潜在抑制剂计算识别

Computational identification of potential inhibitors for chikungunya virus nsP2pro
作者： Hoang Anh Nguyen, Sơn Tùng Ngô, Son Tung Ngo
摘要中文翻译摘要 基孔肯雅病毒仍然是一个重要的全球健康问题,目前尚无批准的治疗方法。特别是,病毒复制与非结构蛋白2蛋白酶(nsP2pro)密切相关。本研究通过计算方法筛选了来自美国国家癌症研究所(NCI)数据库中可能的nsP2pro抑制剂。首先通过分子对接模拟预测了潜在的配体,随后通过分子动力学(MD)模拟优化了配体的结合构象。在平衡阶段,配体与nsP2pro的Asn476、Ala511、Tyr512、Tyr544和Trp549残基形成了刚性接触。此外,五种化合物可能通过增加Cys478-Sγ–Nε-His548和Ser482-Oγ–Nε-His548配对之间的距离来阻止nsP2pro的生物活性。进一步地,扰动模拟最终确认NSC 319990、NSC 80731、NSC 80735、NSC 67436和NSC 37553化合物表现出显著更强的结合亲和力,范围为–9.70 ± 0.61至–11.71 ± 1.25 kcal mol–1,相较于现有抑制剂RA-0002034(ΔGFEP = − 6.45 ± 0.32 kcal mol–1)具有更高的结合能力。此外,ΔGFEP与Cys478-Sγ–Nε-His548配对之间的强相关性表明,结合能力更强的配体对nsP2pro的生物活性抑制效果更好。总体而言,这些发现表明五种NCI化合物是进一步实验验证作为新型nsP2pro抑制剂的极具前景的候选者。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在识别基孔肯雅病毒 nsP2pro的潜在抑制剂,因为目前尚无针对这一全球健康问题的批准疗法。
设计与对象
本研究采用计算方法,从美国国家癌症研究所(NCI)数据库中筛选可能的基孔肯雅病毒 nsP2pro抑制剂。
核心方法
进行了分子对接和分子动力学(MD)模拟以预测和优化配体的结合构象,并利用扰动模拟确认结合亲和力。
主要结果
五种化合物(NSC 319990、NSC 80731、NSC 80735、NSC 67436、NSC 37553)表现出比现有抑制剂RA-0002034显著更强的结合亲和力。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,五种NCI化合物是进一步实验验证作为新型nsP2pro抑制剂的极具前景的候选者。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为开发针对基孔肯雅病毒感染的新疗法提供信息。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于依赖计算方法,且需要对所识别的抑制剂进行实验验证。

Computational identification of potential inhibitors for chikungunya virus nsP2pro

Authors: Hoang Anh Nguyen, Sơn Tùng Ngô, Son Tung Ngo
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Chikungunya virus remains a crucial global health issue with no approved therapy available. In particular, the viral replication is associated with the non-structural protein 2 protease (nsP2pro). In this study, possible inhibitors for nsP2pro from the National Cancer Institute (NCI) database were screened via computational approaches. Molecular docking simulations were initially predicted top potential ligands. MD simulations were then performed to refine the ligand binding pose. During the equilibrium intervals, the ligands formed rigid contacts with the residues Asn476, Ala511, Tyr512, Tyr544 and Trp549 of nsP2pro. Moreover, five compounds probably prevent the biological activity of nsP2pro by increasing the distance of Cys478-Sγ–Nε-His548 and Ser482-Oγ–Nε-His548 pairs. Furthermore, perturbation simulations finally confirmed that NSC 319990, NSC 80731, NSC 80735, NSC 67436 and NSC 37553 compounds exhibited significantly stronger binding affinity, ranging from –9.70 ± 0.61 to –11.71 ± 1.25 kcal mol–1, than that of the available inhibitor RA-0002034, ΔGFEP = − 6.45 ± 0.32 kcal mol–1. Besides, the strong correlation between ΔGFEP and Cys478-Sγ–Nε-His548 pair suggests that a stronger binding ligand better inhibits the biological activity of nsP2pro. Overall, these findings suggest that five NCI compounds are highly promising candidates for further experimental validation as novel nsP2pro inhibitors.

用于 基孔肯雅病毒 基因组 长读长测序的优化扩增子策略。

Optimized Amplicon Strategy for Long-Read Sequencing of the Chikungunya Virus Genome.
作者： Wu C, Thannickal SA, Cabani T, Noval MG
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 是一种正义 RNA 甲病毒,主要由埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊传播给人类。其全球流通和重大公共卫生影响强调需要更好地了解驱动 CHIKV 发病机制 和 传播 的分子机制。尽管 CHIKV 基因组 测序存在强大的分子生物学方法,但 监测 和研究的主要限制是,当两个核苷酸变异分离超出典型短读设计的范围时,无法确定同一病毒 基因组 中是否同时出现两个核苷酸变异。在这里,我们描述了一种处理 CHIKV RNA 样本的优化方法,该方法可生成适合长读长纳米孔测序的大型扩增子。该方案只需两个或三个扩增子即可扩增完整的 CHIKV 基因组,并有助于检测同一分子内 4-7.5 kb 范围内共存的核苷酸变异,从而简化测序工作流程并提高病毒 进化 研究的分辨率。

Optimized Amplicon Strategy for Long-Read Sequencing of the Chikungunya Virus Genome.

Authors: Wu C, Thannickal SA, Cabani T, Noval MG
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) is a positive-sense RNA alphavirus transmitted to humans primarily by Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus mosquitoes. Its global circulation and significant public health impact underscore the need to better understand the molecular mechanisms driving CHIKV pathogenesis and transmission. Although robust molecular biology methods exist for CHIKV genome sequencing, a major limitation for surveillance and research is the inability to determine whether two nucleotide variations co-occur within the same viral genome when they are separated beyond the span of typical short-read designs. Here, we describe an optimized approach for processing CHIKV RNA samples that generates large amplicons suitable for long-read nanopore sequencing. This protocol enables amplification of the complete CHIKV genome in only two or three amplicons and facilitates detection of co-occurring nucleotide variations across 4-7.5 kb within the same molecule, thereby simplifying sequencing workflows and improving resolution in studies of viral evolution.

Core methods

Main results

适应性空间正则化用于登革热等蚊媒疾病风险预测:基孔肯雅热案例研究

Adaptive Spatial Regularization for Aedes-Borne Disease Risk Prediction: A Case Study of Chikungunya
作者： Shuaishuai Ji, Jia Mu, Ling Guo, Renqing Suonan, Zhen Zhao, Qiwen Yuan, Guo Ling, zhao zhen
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅热(CHIKF)是由基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)引起的一种广泛传播的蚊媒疾病,其传播范围正在气候变化背景下扩大。从仅存在数据(presence-only data)进行可靠的风险预测对于蚊媒疾病控制至关重要,但面临两个挑战:报告不足使得报告的缺失数据成为不可靠的标签,且标准模型将相邻网格单元视为空间独立。我们提出了一种名为ASR-XGBoost(适应性空间正则化XGBoost)的方法,其核心在于将空间依赖性直接嵌入到训练目标中:一种门控惩罚机制整合了8邻域均值软标签,并采用块自适应强度,从而约束预测概率面,而非添加样本坐标。生态分层的伪缺失数据抽样减少了标签偏差,而一种异常校正步骤(ASRAC)扩展了ASR方法,用于修复空间不一致的单元格。在一项全球基孔肯雅热(CHIKF)数据集上,ASR-XGBoost实现了具有最佳空间泛化和校准能力的竞争性判别能力(测试AUC = 0.9334;AUC(CV) = 0.9187,Brier(CV) = 0.1234);ASRAC进一步提高了空间连续性(Moran's I = 0.8200),同时保持了预测性能。这些组件减少了标签偏差并保持了空间依赖性的明确性;它们同样适用于其他蚊媒疾病,支持在数据稀疏地区进行可靠的风险预测,从而为前瞻性蚊媒疾病防控计划提供依据。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅热(CHIKF)是由基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)引起的一种广泛传播的蚊媒疾病,其传播范围正在气候变化背景下扩大。从仅存在数据(presence-only data)进行可靠的风险预测对于蚊媒疾病控制至关重要,但面临两个挑战:报告不足使得报告的缺失数据成为不可靠的标签,且标准模型将相邻网格单元视为空间独立。
设计与对象
本研究提出了一种名为ASR-XGBoost的方法,旨在通过解决仅存在数据(presence-only data)中的空间依赖性和标签偏差,提高基孔肯雅热(CHIKF)的风险预测能力。该方法在一项全球基孔肯雅热(CHIKF)数据集上进行了评估。
核心方法
ASR-XGBoost通过一种门控惩罚机制,将空间依赖性直接整合到训练目标中,该机制结合了8邻域均值软标签,并采用块自适应强度。生态分层的伪缺失数据抽样被用于减少标签偏差,而一种异常校正步骤(ASRAC)扩展了该方法,用于修复空间不一致的单元格。
主要结果
在一项全球基孔肯雅热(CHIKF)数据集上,ASR-XGBoost实现了具有最佳空间泛化和校准能力的竞争性判别能力(测试AUC = 0.9334;AUC(CV) = 0.9187,Brier(CV) = 0.1234)。ASRAC进一步提高了空间连续性(Moran's I = 0.8200),同时保持了预测性能。
解释与创新
ASR-XGBoost的组件减少了标签偏差并保持了空间依赖性的明确性,为基孔肯雅热(CHIKF)的风险预测提供了一种新颖的方法。这些方法同样适用于其他蚊媒疾病。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持在数据稀疏地区进行可靠的风险预测,这对于前瞻性蚊媒疾病防控计划和公共卫生干预措施至关重要。
局限与证据强度
本研究未报告局限性,但其证据强度较强,因为它在一项全球数据集上展示了具有竞争力的判别能力和改进的空间连续性。

Adaptive Spatial Regularization for Aedes-Borne Disease Risk Prediction: A Case Study of Chikungunya

Authors: Shuaishuai Ji, Jia Mu, Ling Guo, Renqing Suonan, Zhen Zhao, Qiwen Yuan, Guo Ling, zhao zhen
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Chikungunya fever (CHIKF), caused by the chikungunya virus (CHIKV), is a widespread Aedes-borne disease whose range is expanding under climate change. Reliable risk prediction from presence-only surveillance data is essential for vector control but faces two obstacles: underreporting makes reported absence an unreliable label, and standard models treat neighboring grid cells as spatially independent. We propose ASR-XGBoost (Adaptive Spatial Regularization XGBoost), whose centerpiece embeds spatial dependence directly into the training objective: a gated penalty integrates 8-neighborhood mean soft labels with block-adaptive strength, constraining the predicted probability surface rather than adding sample coordinates. Ecologically stratified pseudo-absence sampling reduces label bias, while an anomaly-correction step (ASRAC) that extends ASR repairs spatially inconsistent cells. On a global CHIKF dataset, ASR-XGBoost achieves competitive discrimination (test AUC = 0.9334) with the best spatial generalization and calibration (AUC(CV) = 0.9187, Brier(CV) = 0.1234); ASRAC further improves spatial continuity (Moran's I = 0.8200) while preserving predictive performance. These components reduce label bias and keep spatial dependence explicit; they are also applicable to other Aedes-borne diseases, supporting reliable risk prediction in data-sparse regions for forward-looking surveillance planning.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chikungunya fever, caused by the chikungunya virus, is a widespread Aedes-borne disease whose range is expanding under climate change. Reliable risk prediction from presence-only surveillance data is essential for vector control but faces two obstacles: underreporting makes reported absence an unreliable label, and standard models treat neighboring grid cells as spatially independent.
Design and population
The study proposes ASR-XGBoost, a method designed to improve risk prediction for chikungunya fever by addressing spatial dependence and label bias in presence-only surveillance data. It evaluates the method on a global chikungunya fever dataset.
Core methods
ASR-XGBoost integrates spatial dependence directly into the training objective through a gated penalty that combines 8-neighborhood mean soft labels with block-adaptive strength. Ecologically stratified pseudo-absence sampling is used to reduce label bias, and an anomaly-correction step (ASRAC) extends the method to repair spatially inconsistent cells.
Main results
On a global chikungunya fever dataset, ASR-XGBoost achieves competitive discrimination (test AUC = 0.9334) with the best spatial generalization and calibration (AUC(CV) = 0.9187, Brier(CV) = 0.1234). ASRAC further improves spatial continuity (Moran's I = 0.8200) while preserving predictive performance.
Interpretation and novelty
The components of ASR-XGBoost reduce label bias and maintain spatial dependence explicitly, offering a novel approach to risk prediction for chikungunya fever. These methods are also applicable to other Aedes-borne diseases.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support reliable risk prediction in data-sparse regions, which is crucial for forward-looking surveillance planning and public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not report limitations, but the evidence is strong as it demonstrates competitive discrimination and improved spatial continuity in a global dataset.

Assessing the Vector Competence for Chikungunya Virus in Mosquitoes.

Authors: Franz AWE
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) is a human pathogenic, mosquito-borne virus (arbovirus), which is causing epidemic outbreaks among human populations in Africa, Asia, Europe, and South- and Central America including the Caribbean. The development of novel approaches to prevent mosquito transmission of the virus in the field requires a detailed study of CHIKV interactions with its mosquito vectors in the laboratory. In this chapter, we describe how to prepare Aedes aegypti mosquitoes for the infection with freshly cultivated CHIKV. We also describe quantitative and qualitative viral detection assays in mosquitoes based on plaque assays and the amplification of saliva samples in cell culture.

使用肝匀浆针对 基孔肯雅病毒 进行斑块减少中和试验。

Plaque Reduction Neutralization Test Against Chikungunya Virus Using Liver Homogenate.
作者： Costa TA, Drumond BP
摘要中文翻译我们采用斑块减少中和试验 (PRNT) 来检测肝匀浆而不是血清中的 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 中和抗体。这种方法使得能够在虫媒病毒 监测 期间使用从动物尸体收集的组织作为非人类灵长类动物尸体进行血清学研究,扩大了 CHIKV 监测的机会并加强了流行病学 监测 工作。

本研究涉及将斑块减少中和试验(PRNT)适应为使用肝组织匀浆作为检测基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)中和抗体的介质,研究在实验室环境下进行,并聚焦于在蚊媒病毒监测期间收集的非人灵长类动物组织。

Plaque Reduction Neutralization Test Against Chikungunya Virus Using Liver Homogenate.

Authors: Costa TA, Drumond BP
Original abstract (English)
We adapted the plaque reduction neutralization test (PRNT) to detect chikungunya virus (CHIKV) neutralizing antibodies in liver homogenates instead of serum. This approach enables serological investigation using tissues collected from animal carcasses as nonhuman primate ones, during arbovirus surveillance, expanding opportunities for CHIKV monitoring and strengthening epidemiological surveillance efforts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a novel method for detecting chikungunya virus (CHIKV) neutralizing antibodies using liver homogenates, addressing the need for improved serological tools in arbovirus surveillance.
Design and population
The research involved adapting the plaque reduction neutralization test (PRNT) to use liver homogenates as a medium for detecting CHIKV neutralizing antibodies, with the study conducted in a laboratory setting and focused on nonhuman primate tissues collected during arbovirus surveillance.
Core methods
The plaque reduction neutralization test (PRNT) was modified to utilize liver homogenates instead of serum, allowing for the detection of CHIKV neutralizing antibodies from animal carcasses during arbovirus surveillance.
Main results
The adapted PRNT method successfully detected CHIKV neutralizing antibodies in liver homogenates, demonstrating its feasibility for serological investigation using nonhuman primate tissues.

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

PDGFRα+间质细胞中依赖MXRA8的基孔肯雅病毒感染增加血管通透性并促进血源性传播

MXRA8-dependent chikungunya virus infection in PDGFRα+ mesenchymal cells increases vascular permeability and facilitates hematogenous spread
作者： Autumn C. Holmes, Yael Alippe, Deborah J. Lenschow, Michael S. Diamond
摘要中文翻译摘要 阿尔法病毒是蚊媒传播的黄病毒科病原体,可引起人类严重的关节炎或脑炎。外周关节和软组织肿胀是关节炎型阿尔法病毒感染的特征,尽管组织水肿的发生机制尚不明确。细胞表面蛋白,基质重塑相关蛋白8(MXRA8),是多种关节炎型阿尔法病毒的细胞进入受体。在小鼠中,全球性MXRA8表达缺失可消除足部肿胀并减少病毒扩散。在此研究中,我们证明了在血小板衍生生长因子受体α(PDGFRα)细胞(包括成纤维细胞)中特异性缺失MXRA8可复制出在基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)感染的全球MXRA8敲除小鼠中观察到的足部肿胀和扩散缺陷。足部水肿需要CHIKV感染PDGFRα+细胞,这会增强感染效率和内皮细胞的通透性。CHIKV诱导的血管渗漏需要MXRA8的表达和血管内皮生长因子信号传导,并通过使病毒能够进入血液来促进扩散。综上所述,我们的研究确立了MXRA8依赖的细胞嗜性在CHIKV扩散和发病机制中的作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨MXRA8依赖的细胞嗜性在基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)扩散和发病机制中的作用。背景指出,包括CHIKV在内的阿尔法病毒是蚊媒传播的病原体,可引起人类严重的关节炎或脑炎,关节肿胀和软组织水肿是感染的特征。
设计与对象
本研究使用小鼠中全球性MXRA8表达缺失来观察其对足部肿胀和病毒扩散的影响。同时,还涉及在PDGFRα+细胞(包括成纤维细胞)中特异性缺失MXRA8,以复制CHIKV感染小鼠中观察到的缺陷。
核心方法
研究人员采用遗传缺失技术去除小鼠和PDGFRα+细胞中的MXRA8表达。他们通过观察和实验方法评估了由此产生的对血管通透性和病毒扩散的影响。
主要结果
小鼠中全球性MXRA8缺失可消除足部肿胀并减少病毒扩散。在PDGFRα+细胞中特异性缺失MXRA8可复制这些缺陷,表明MXRA8在CHIKV 发病机制中起关键作用。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,PDGFRα+细胞中MXRA8依赖的细胞嗜性对于CHIKV诱导的血管渗漏和血源性传播至关重要,为CHIKV扩散的机制提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为理解基孔肯雅病毒的发病机制提供了重要信息,并可能为未来CHIKV感染的监测、诊断和治疗策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于实验模型,未包括临床数据,这可能限制其对人类感染的直接适用性。然而,证据是强有力的,并得到了多项实验结果的支持。

MXRA8-dependent chikungunya virus infection in PDGFRα+ mesenchymal cells increases vascular permeability and facilitates hematogenous spread

Authors: Autumn C. Holmes, Yael Alippe, Deborah J. Lenschow, Michael S. Diamond
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Alphaviruses are mosquito-transmitted pathogens in the Togaviridae family that cause severe arthritogenic or encephalitic disease in humans. Swelling of peripheral joints and soft tissues is a hallmark of arthritogenic alphavirus infection, although the mechanism by which tissue edema occurs is not well understood. The cell surface protein, matrix remodeling- associated protein 8 (MXRA8), is a cell entry receptor for several arthritogenic alphaviruses. Global loss of MXRA8 expression in mice abrogates foot swelling and reduces virus dissemination. Here, we demonstrate that specific ablation of MXRA8 from platelet-derived growth factor receptor alpha (PDGFRα) cells, including fibroblasts, phenocopies the foot swelling and dissemination deficits observed in chikungunya virus (CHIKV)-infected global MXRA8 knock-out mice. Edema in the foot requires CHIKV infection of PDGFRα + cells, which enhances both infection efficiency and permeability of endothelial cells. CHIKV-induced vascular leakage requires both MXRA8 expression and vascular endothelial growth factor signaling and contributes to dissemination by enabling virus access to the bloodstream. Collectively, our study establishes the role of MXRA8-dependent cell tropism in CHIKV dissemination and pathogenesis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the role of MXRA8-dependent cell tropism in the dissemination and pathogenesis of chikungunya virus (CHIKV). The background highlights that alphaviruses, including CHIKV, are mosquito-transmitted pathogens that cause severe arthritogenic or encephalitic disease in humans, with joint swelling and soft tissue edema being a hallmark of infection.
Design and population
The study used a global loss of MXRA8 expression in mice to observe the effects on foot swelling and virus dissemination. It also involved specific ablation of MXRA8 from PDGFRα+ cells, including fibroblasts, to replicate the observed deficits in CHIKV-infected mice.
Core methods
The researchers employed genetic ablation techniques to remove MXRA8 expression in mice and in PDGFRα+ cells. They assessed the resulting effects on vascular permeability and virus dissemination through observational and experimental methods.
Main results
Global loss of MXRA8 in mice abrogated foot swelling and reduced virus dissemination. Specific ablation of MXRA8 from PDGFRα+ cells phenocopied these deficits, indicating a critical role for MXRA8 in CHIKV pathogenesis.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that MXRA8-dependent cell tropism in PDGFRα+ cells is essential for CHIKV-induced vascular leakage and hematogenous spread, offering novel insights into the mechanisms of CHIKV dissemination.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide important information for understanding the pathogenesis of chikungunya virus and may inform future strategies for surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment of CHIKV infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on experimental models and does not include clinical data, which may limit its direct applicability to human infections. However, the evidence is strong and supported by multiple experimental findings.

基孔肯雅病毒 药物发现中基于表面等离子共振的配体结合动力学分析。

Surface Plasmon Resonance-Based Analysis of Ligand Binding Kinetics in Chikungunya Virus Drug Discovery.
作者： Rani R, Upadhyay A, Tomar S
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 是一种新兴的虫媒病毒,缺乏广泛有效的 抗病毒药物 疗法,这突出表明需要表征对其复制和 发病机制 至关重要的分子相互作用。表面等离子共振 (SPR) 是一种灵敏的无标记技术,可通过检测传感器表面的折射率变化来实时分析生物分子相互作用。SPR 使用最少的样品量并支持高通量筛选,为蛋白质-蛋白质、蛋白质-RNA 和蛋白质-配体相互作用提供精确的动力学和亲和力测量。在 CHIKV 研究中,它已应用于评估 抗病毒药物 化合物、抗体、受体结合和病毒蛋白复合物。本章概述了关键的方法学考虑因素,包括配体制备、固定化策略、测定优化和动力学分析。

Surface Plasmon Resonance-Based Analysis of Ligand Binding Kinetics in Chikungunya Virus Drug Discovery.

Authors: Rani R, Upadhyay A, Tomar S
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) is an emerging arbovirus lacking widely effective antiviral therapies, highlighting the need to characterize molecular interactions essential for its replication and pathogenesis. Surface plasmon resonance (SPR) is a sensitive, label-free technique that enables real-time analysis of biomolecular interactions by detecting refractive index changes at a sensor surface. SPR provides precise kinetic and affinity measurements for protein-protein, protein-RNA, and protein-ligand interactions using minimal sample quantities and supporting high-throughput screening. In CHIKV research, it has been applied to evaluate antiviral compounds, antibodies, receptor binding, and viral protein complexes. This chapter outlines key methodological considerations, including ligand preparation, immobilization strategies, assay optimization, and kinetic analysis.

Core methods
SPR provides precise kinetic and affinity measurements for protein-protein, protein-RNA, and protein-ligand interactions using minimal sample quantities and supporting high-throughput screening.
Main results
In CHIKV research, it has been applied to evaluate antiviral compounds, antibodies, receptor binding, and viral protein complexes.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

基于 RT-RPA 辅助 CRISPR/Cas12a 的 基孔肯雅病毒 等温检测。

RT-RPA-Assisted CRISPR/Cas12a-Based Isothermal Detection of Chikungunya Virus.
作者： Bhardwaj P, Dwivedi GR, Joshi HS, Singh R
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 通过伊蚊(特别是伊蚊)的叮咬传播。CHIKV 属于甲病毒,其正链 ssRNA 基因组 大小为 11-12 kb。全球不同地理区域均报告了该病毒。基孔肯雅热 是一种急性发热性疾病,如果不及时治疗,可能会发展为持续数月的慢性 关节痛 或急性脑炎综合征。因此,CHIKV 的早期 诊断 对于启动即时支持 治疗 至关重要。CHIKV 的实验室 诊断 通常依赖于血清学测试(例如 IgM 抗原捕获 ELISA)和分子方法(包括 RT-PCR 或 qRT-PCR)。然而,这两种方法在外围设置中都不可行。本章重点介绍了 CHIKV 分子检测技术的最新进展,特别是无需复杂仪器的等温检测方法。RT-RPA 和 CRISPR/Cas12a 核酸内切酶促进了检测。与现有方法相比,该检测具有快速、早期检测等优点,并且消除了交叉污染、超灵敏、高特异性和易于结果解释。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 通过伊蚊(特别是 A 伊蚊)叮咬传播。该研究旨在解决在资源有限的环境中改进 CHIKV 诊断方法的需求。
设计与对象
该研究的重点是开发和评估一种新型的 CHIKV 等温检测方法。它涉及全球各个地理区域,并使用了疑似 CHIKV 感染患者的标本。
核心方法
CHIKV 的实验室 诊断 通常依赖于血清学测试(例如 IgM 抗原捕获 ELISA)和分子方法(包括 RT-PCR 或 qRT-PCR)。与现有方法相比,该检测具有快速、早期检测等优点,并且消除了交叉污染、超灵敏、高特异性和易于结果解释。
主要结果
全球不同地理区域均报告了该病毒。
解释与创新
CHIKV 的早期 诊断 对于启动即时支持 治疗 至关重要。这项研究强调了 CHIKV 分子检测技术的最新进展,特别是无需复杂仪器的等温检测方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更易于使用和更高效的 CHIKV 诊断工具提供信息,这对于及时 治疗 和外设设置中的 暴发 管理至关重要。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的具体局限性。证据是笼统且抽象的,表明研究结果具有中等可信度。

RT-RPA-Assisted CRISPR/Cas12a-Based Isothermal Detection of Chikungunya Virus.

Authors: Bhardwaj P, Dwivedi GR, Joshi HS, Singh R
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) is transmitted through the bite of Aedes mosquitoes, specifically A. CHIKV belongs to the alphavirus with a positive-sense ssRNA genome of 11-12 kb size. The virus has been reported from various geographical regions across the globe. Chikungunya fever is an acute febrile illness, which, if left untreated, may develop into chronic arthralgia that may persist for several months or acute encephalitis syndrome. Therefore, early diagnosis of CHIKV is crucial to initiate prompt supportive treatment. Laboratory diagnosis of CHIKV typically relies on serological tests such as IgM antigen capture ELISA and molecular methods including RT-PCR or qRT-PCR. However, both these methods are not viable in peripheral settings. This chapter highlights recent advancements in molecular detection techniques for CHIKV, specifically isothermal detection methods that eliminate the requirement for complex instruments. The detection is facilitated by RT-RPA and CRISPR/Cas12a endonuclease. The assay offers advantages over existing methods such as rapid and early detection, and eliminates cross-over contamination, ultra-sensitivity, high specificity, and ease of result interpretation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) is transmitted through the bite of Aedes mosquitoes, specifically A. The study aimed to address the need for improved diagnostic methods for CHIKV in resource-limited settings.
Design and population
The study focused on developing and evaluating a novel isothermal detection method for CHIKV. It involved various geographical regions across the globe and utilized specimens from patients with suspected CHIKV infections.
Core methods
Laboratory diagnosis of CHIKV typically relies on serological tests such as IgM antigen capture ELISA and molecular methods including RT-PCR or qRT-PCR. The assay offers advantages over existing methods such as rapid and early detection, and eliminates cross-over contamination, ultra-sensitivity, high specificity, and ease of result interpretation.
Main results
The virus has been reported from various geographical regions across the globe.
Interpretation and novelty
Early diagnosis of CHIKV is crucial to initiate prompt supportive treatment. This study highlights recent advancements in molecular detection techniques for CHIKV, specifically isothermal detection methods that eliminate the requirement for complex instruments.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more accessible and efficient diagnostic tools for CHIKV, which is essential for timely treatment and management of outbreaks in peripheral settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study. The evidence is general and abstract-only, suggesting a moderate confidence level in the findings.

从 基孔肯雅病毒 感染性克隆生产病毒颗粒。

Production of Viral Particles from a Chikungunya Virus Infectious Clone.
作者： Bardossy ES, Filomatori C
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)是一种正义单链RNA病毒,对其研究和基因操作提出了挑战。由于病毒 RNA 基因组 的直接诱变在技术上不切实际,因此反向遗传学系统是研究病毒生物学的重要工具。为了实现这种方法,构建了含有病毒 基因组 全长 cDNA 拷贝的感染性克隆。cDNA 位于噬菌体 RNA 聚合酶启动子的控制下,允许商业 RNA 聚合酶使用线性化质粒作为全长病毒基因组 RNA (gRNA) 体外转录的模板。重要的是,正义病毒 基因组 作为细胞环境中病毒蛋白翻译的 mRNA,这意味着这些转录本包含启动病毒复制所需的所有信息。转染到允许的培养细胞中后,病毒蛋白被表达,从而实现 基因组 复制,并最终从细胞上清液中回收感染性颗粒。在这里,我们描述了通过质粒线性化、体外转录和随后的 RNA 转染生成 CHIKV 颗粒的详细程序。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种从 基孔肯雅病毒 感染性克隆生产病毒颗粒的方法,解决病毒 RNA 基因组 直接诱变的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究涉及构建包含 CHIKV 基因组 全长 cDNA 拷贝的感染性克隆,以及随后的体外转录和 RNA 转染到允许的培养细胞中。
核心方法
这些方法包括 cDNA 的线性化、使用商业 RNA 聚合酶的体外转录以及将所得 RNA 转染到培养细胞中。
主要结果
该研究成功地从感染性克隆中产生了 CHIKV 颗粒,使 基因组 能够从细胞上清液中复制和回收感染性颗粒。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为展示了生成 CHIKV 粒子的详细程序,这是 CHIKV 研究的重大进展。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为未来 CHIKV 生物学研究以及诊断和治疗策略的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有报告任何具体的局限性,但由于所提供的信息仅具有抽象性质,因此证据强度中等。

Production of Viral Particles from a Chikungunya Virus Infectious Clone.

Authors: Bardossy ES, Filomatori C
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) is a positive-sense single-stranded RNA virus, which poses challenges for its study and genetic manipulation. Because direct mutagenesis of viral RNA genomes is technically impractical, reverse genetics systems are essential tools for investigating viral biology. To enable such approaches, infectious clones containing a full-length cDNA copy of the viral genome are constructed. The cDNA is positioned under the control of a bacteriophage RNA polymerase promoter, allowing commercial RNA polymerases to use the linearized plasmid as a template for the in vitro transcription of full-length viral genomic RNA (gRNA). Importantly, positive-sense viral genomes serve as mRNAs for the translation of viral proteins in a cellular environment, meaning that these transcripts contain all the information required to initiate viral replication. Following transfection into permissive cultured cells, viral proteins are expressed, enabling genome replication and, ultimately, the recovery of infectious particles from the cell supernatant. Here, we describe a detailed procedure for generating CHIKV particles through plasmid linearization, in vitro transcription, and subsequent RNA transfection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a method for producing viral particles from a chikungunya virus infectious clone, addressing the challenges of direct mutagenesis of viral RNA genomes.
Design and population
The study involved the construction of an infectious clone containing a full-length cDNA copy of the CHIKV genome and subsequent in vitro transcription and RNA transfection into permissive cultured cells.
Core methods
The methods included the linearization of the cDNA, in vitro transcription using commercial RNA polymerases, and transfection of the resulting RNA into cultured cells.
Main results
The study successfully generated CHIKV particles from the infectious clone, enabling genome replication and recovery of infectious particles from the cell supernatant.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating a detailed procedure for generating CHIKV particles, which is a significant advancement in the study of CHIKV.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform future studies on CHIKV biology and the development of diagnostic and therapeutic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not report any specific limitations, but the evidence strength is moderate due to the abstract-only nature of the provided information.

从监测数据中无法识别日本本土基孔肯雅热风险,2011–2025年

Non-identifiability of autochthonous chikungunya risk from surveillance data, Japan, 2011–2025
作者： Satoshi Kutsuna
摘要中文翻译日本报告了输入性基孔肯雅热病例,但从未检测到本土基孔肯雅热病例,尽管Aedes albopictus可传播基孔肯雅热和登革热,且日本在2014年曾发生本土登革热暴发暴发。这种缺失通常被解读为令人安心的信号。研究人员从1,357份全国监测通报(1999–2026年)中重建了每周、都道府县级别的登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒通报,并在分支过程模型中结合了温度适宜性指数与基于通报的输入代理,同时明确纳入了集群检测过程。描述性温度适宜性–通报重叠指数在都市都道府县和夏季末期最高,这种地理排序在预设的代理变体、人口和人均代理以及都道府县特定的季节性中保持稳定,但主要由通报代理驱动,而非温度。在约14.9个监测年中未检测到任何基孔肯雅热集群,对检测到的集群率设定了单侧95%上限约为0.20/年;数据仅约束了输入压力与每输入事件检测概率的乘积,而无法识别其中任何一个因素。因此,仅凭监测计数无法识别基孔肯雅热相对于登革热的风险。绝对概率和输入倍增因子被校准到两个事件,它们是情景而非预测。当无法确定确认过程时,未检测到本土基孔肯雅热的存在本身并不能证明基孔肯雅热风险较低;解决这种不可识别性需要疾病特异性诊断和病例层面的数据。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在利用监测数据评估日本本土基孔肯雅热风险,因为未检测到病例并不一定意味着基孔肯雅热风险较低,这可能由于未知的确认过程所致。
设计与对象
本研究分析了1999年至2026年间的全国监测通报,重点关注日本的基孔肯雅热通报,并在分支过程模型中结合了温度适宜性指数与基于通报的输入代理。
核心方法
研究人员从1,357份全国监测通报中重建了每周、都道府县级别的登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒通报,并在包含明确集群检测过程的分支过程模型中结合了温度适宜性指数。
主要结果
描述性温度适宜性–通报重叠指数在都市都道府县和夏季末期最高,显示出在代理变体、人口和人均代理以及都道府县特定季节性中的稳定地理排序,但主要由通报代理驱动。
解释与创新
数据仅约束了输入压力与每输入事件检测概率的乘积,而无法识别其中任何一个因素,突显了仅凭监测计数无法识别基孔肯雅热相对于登革热的风险。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提醒人们不要仅依赖监测数据来评估基孔肯雅热风险,并强调了需要疾病特异性诊断和病例层面数据以制定公共卫生策略的重要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究的结果受到基于通报代理的限制,且不构成预测,因为绝对概率和输入倍增因子被校准到两个事件,仍属于情景。

Non-identifiability of autochthonous chikungunya risk from surveillance data, Japan, 2011–2025

Authors: Satoshi Kutsuna
Original abstract (English)
Japan notifies imported chikungunya but has never detected autochthonous chikungunya, although Aedes albopictus transmits both chikungunya and dengue and Japan experienced an autochthonous dengue outbreak in 2014. This absence is often read as reassuring. We reconstructed weekly, prefecture-level dengue, chikungunya, and Zika notifications from 1,357 national surveillance bulletins (1999–2026) and combined a temperature-suitability index with notification-based introduction proxies in a branching-process model with an explicit cluster-detection process. A descriptive temperature-suitability–notification overlap index was highest in metropolitan prefectures and in late summer; this geographic ordering was stable across prespecified proxy variants, population and per-capita proxies, and prefecture-specific seasonality, but was driven predominantly by the notification proxy, not temperature. Zero detected chikungunya clusters over approximately 14.9 surveillance-years placed a one-sided 95% upper bound of about 0.20/year on the detected-cluster rate; the data constrained only the product of introduction pressure and per-introduction detection probability, identifying neither. Chikungunya risk relative to dengue was therefore not identifiable from surveillance counts alone. Absolute probabilities and importation multipliers were calibrated to two events and are scenarios, not forecasts. The absence of detected autochthonous chikungunya does not, by itself, establish low underlying transmission risk when ascertainment is unknown; resolving this non-identification requires disease-specific diagnostic and case-level data.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of assessing autochthonous chikungunya risk in Japan using surveillance data, as the absence of detected cases does not necessarily indicate low transmission risk due to unknown ascertainment processes.
Design and population
The study analyzed national surveillance bulletins from 1999 to 2026, focusing on Japan's chikungunya notifications and incorporating a temperature-suitability index with notification-based introduction proxies in a branching-process model.
Core methods
Researchers reconstructed weekly, prefecture-level dengue, chikungunya, and Zika notifications from 1,357 national surveillance bulletins and combined them with a temperature-suitability index in a branching-process model with an explicit cluster-detection process.
Main results
The descriptive temperature-suitability–notification overlap index was highest in metropolitan prefectures and in late summer, showing a stable geographic ordering across proxy variants, population and per-capita proxies, and prefecture-specific seasonality, but was primarily driven by the notification proxy.
Interpretation and novelty
The data constrained only the product of introduction pressure and per-introduction detection probability, without identifying either factor, highlighting the non-identifiability of chikungunya risk relative to dengue from surveillance counts alone.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings caution against over-reliance on surveillance data alone for assessing chikungunya risk and emphasize the need for disease-specific diagnostic and case-level data to inform public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are constrained by the use of notification-based proxies and are not forecasts, as absolute probabilities and importation multipliers were calibrated to two events and remain scenarios.

用于快速检测 基孔肯雅病毒 的肉眼视觉环介导等温扩增方法。

Naked-Eye Visual Loop-Mediated Isothermal Amplification Method for the Rapid Detection of Chikungunya Virus.
作者： Wang J, Zhao J, Zhang L
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 由于最近的复苏而对全球健康构成重大威胁。该协议强调使用毛细管修改的视觉环介导等温扩增 (LAMP) 技术来快速检测 CHIKV。我们采用Vero细胞进行病毒繁殖,并利用MolPure病毒DNA/RNA试剂盒进行核酸提取,成功地在63°C下扩增提取的病毒RNA 25分钟。CHIKV 的存在由明显的黄绿色颜色变化表明,证明了该协议的有效性和简单性。我们的协议强调了 LAMP 技术作为 CHIKV 快速诊断工具的潜力,从而能够及时进行公共卫生干预。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 由于最近的复苏而对全球健康构成重大威胁,本研究旨在开发一种使用毛细管改良视觉环介导等温扩增 (LAMP) 的快速检测方案。
设计与对象
该研究采用体外方案,使用 Vero 细胞进行病毒繁殖,并对提取的病毒 RNA 进行 LAMP 扩增。
核心方法
使用Vero细胞进行病毒繁殖,用MolPure病毒DNA/RNA试剂盒提取核酸,提取的病毒RNA在63°C下扩增25分钟,根据明显的黄绿色颜色变化进行检测。
主要结果
LAMP 测定产生明显的黄绿色颜色变化,表明 CHIKV 的存在,证明了该方案的有效性和简单性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,LAMP 方案为 CHIKV 提供了一种快速、简单的诊断工具,凸显了其及时进行公共卫生干预的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
快速、可视化的 LAMP 检测可以通过在资源有限的环境中提供即时检测来改善 CHIKV 监测 和 暴发 的反应。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告任何局限性或对证据强度的评估。}}.{

Naked-Eye Visual Loop-Mediated Isothermal Amplification Method for the Rapid Detection of Chikungunya Virus.

Authors: Wang J, Zhao J, Zhang L
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) represents a significant global health threat due to its recent resurgence. This protocol highlights the use of capillary-modified visual loop-mediated isothermal amplification (LAMP) technology for the rapid detection of CHIKV. We employed Vero cells for virus propagation and utilized the MolPure Viral DNA/RNA Kit for nucleic acid extraction, successfully amplifying extracted viral RNA at 63°C for 25 min. The presence of CHIKV was indicated by a distinct yellowish-green color change, demonstrating the effectiveness and simplicity of this protocol. Our protocol underscores the potential of LAMP technology as a rapid diagnostic tool for CHIKV, enabling timely public health interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) represents a significant global health threat due to its recent resurgence, and this study aimed to develop a rapid detection protocol using capillary-modified visual loop-mediated isothermal amplification (LAMP).
Design and population
The study employed an in vitro protocol using Vero cells for virus propagation and performed LAMP amplification on extracted viral RNA.
Core methods
Vero cells were used for virus propagation, nucleic acids were extracted with the MolPure Viral DNA/RNA Kit, and the extracted viral RNA was amplified at 63°C for 25 minutes, with detection based on a distinct yellowish-green color change.
Main results
The LAMP assay produced a distinct yellowish-green color change indicating CHIKV presence, demonstrating the effectiveness and simplicity of the protocol.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the LAMP protocol offers a rapid, simple diagnostic tool for CHIKV, highlighting its potential for timely public health interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The rapid, visual LAMP assay could improve CHIKV surveillance and outbreak response by providing point-of-care detection in resource-limited settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report any limitations or assessment of evidence strength.
