基孔肯雅热 抗病毒药物 的计算机药物设计:使用共识方法进行虚拟筛选。

In Silico Drug Design of Chikungunya Antivirals: Using Consensus Methods for Virtual Screening.
作者： Battini L
摘要中文翻译基于结构的虚拟筛选是药物设计中一种有吸引力的策略。它能够选择潜在的结合剂来靶向具有已知分子结构的生物分子,为研究其作用机制并指导进一步优化提供合理的框架。然而,准确预测结合自由能仍然是一项极具挑战性的任务,并且可用的工具只能提供近似估计。在这种情况下,提高虚拟筛选的预测能力并增加识别真正结合物的概率的一种策略是采用多种对接算法并使用共识方法组合其结果。在本章中,我们将描述执行此任务的分步方法。我们将使用 AutoDock Vina、AutoDock4 和 LeDock 进行虚拟筛选,然后使用指数共识排名算法合并它们的结果。最后,我们将讨论该策略的可能扩展,例如将分子动力学模拟作为虚拟筛选中的附加过滤器,或将基于人工智能的新颖方法集成到共识框架中。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决 基孔肯雅病毒 药物设计中准确预测结合自由能的挑战,因为基于结构的虚拟筛选为研究作用机制和指导优化提供了合理的框架。
设计与对象
该研究描述了一种使用 AutoDock Vina、AutoDock4 和 LeDock 的虚拟筛选方法,并将其结果与指数共识排名算法相结合,以提高对真实结合物的识别。
核心方法
这些方法涉及使用三种对接算法进行虚拟筛选,并通过共识方法结合其结果,以增强筛选过程的预测能力。
主要结果
该研究概述了执行虚拟筛选和基于共识的分析的分步方法,以确定 基孔肯雅病毒 的潜在 抗病毒药物。
解释与创新
作者建议对该策略进行可能的扩展,例如将分子动力学模拟或基于人工智能的方法纳入共识框架中,以进一步完善虚拟筛选过程。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 基孔肯雅病毒 开发更有效的计算机药物设计策略提供信息,从而有可能加速 抗病毒药物 的发现和预防。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于方法的抽象级别描述,并且不提供实证结果,导致对所提出的方法进行适度的置信度评估。

In Silico Drug Design of Chikungunya Antivirals: Using Consensus Methods for Virtual Screening.

Authors: Battini L
Original abstract (English)
Structure-based virtual screening is an attractive strategy in drug design. It enables the selection of potential binders to target biomolecules with known molecular structures, providing a rational framework for studying their mechanisms of action and guiding further optimization. However, accurately predicting the free energy of binding remains an extremely challenging task, and the available tools only provide approximate estimates. In this context, one strategy to improve the predictive power of virtual screening and increase the probability of identifying true binders is to employ multiple docking algorithms and combine their results using a consensus method. In this chapter, we will describe a step-by-step methodology to perform this task. We will conduct a virtual screening using AutoDock Vina, AutoDock4, and LeDock, and then combine their results employing the Exponential Consensus Ranking algorithm. Finally, we will discuss possible extensions of this strategy, such as incorporating Molecular Dynamics simulations as an additional filter in the virtual screening or integrating the novel Artificial Intelligence-based methodologies into the consensus framework.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to address the challenge of accurately predicting the free energy of binding in drug design for Chikungunya virus, as structure-based virtual screening provides a rational framework for studying mechanisms of action and guiding optimization.
Design and population
The study describes a virtual screening approach using AutoDock Vina, AutoDock4, and LeDock, and combines their results with the Exponential Consensus Ranking algorithm to improve the identification of true binders.
Core methods
The methods involved conducting a virtual screening using three docking algorithms and combining their results through a consensus method to enhance the predictive power of the screening process.
Main results
The study outlines a step-by-step methodology for performing virtual screening and consensus-based analysis to identify potential antivirals for Chikungunya virus.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors suggest possible extensions of the strategy, such as incorporating Molecular Dynamics simulations or AI-based methodologies into the consensus framework, to further refine the virtual screening process.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more effective in silico drug design strategies for Chikungunya virus, potentially accelerating the discovery of antivirals for treatment and prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-level description of the methodology and does not provide empirical results, leading to a moderate confidence assessment in the proposed approach.

基孔肯雅病毒 nsP2 蛋白酶的表达和纯化。

Expression and Purification of nsP2 Protease of Chikungunya Virus.
作者： Barraza Sánchez JJ, Faraj SE
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 的非结构蛋白 2 (nsP2) 是一种重要的半胱氨酸蛋白酶,参与病毒多蛋白加工和复制复合物调节。nsP2 的生化和结构研究需要高纯度且具有酶活性的蛋白质。在本章中,我们描述了在大肠杆菌中异源表达和纯化 CHIKV nsP2 蛋白酶结构域 (nsP2pro) 的简单方案。该蛋白质在低温下表达为 His 标记的构建体,并在天然条件下通过固定金属离子亲和层析进行纯化,然后进行透析以进行缓冲液交换。该程序通常会产生毫克量的高纯度 (>95%) 和活性 nsP2pro,并已成功应用于野生型和突变体的酶测定和抑制剂筛选。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种表达和纯化 基孔肯雅病毒 nsP2 蛋白酶的方法,这对于病毒复制和多蛋白加工至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究描述了在大肠杆菌中异源表达和纯化 CHIKV nsP2 蛋白酶结构域的方案,重点是蛋白质的生产和分离。
核心方法
nsP2 蛋白酶结构域在低温下表达为 His 标记构建体,并在天然条件下使用固定化金属离子亲和层析进行纯化,然后进行透析以进行缓冲液交换。
主要结果
该程序通常会产生毫克量的高纯度 (>95%) 和活性 nsP2pro,并已成功应用于野生型和突变体的酶测定和抑制剂筛选。
解释与创新
所述方法为获得活性 nsP2pro 提供了一种可靠且直接的方法,这对于进一步的生化和结构研究很有价值。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持开发用于研究 CHIKV 蛋白酶功能的工具,这可能有助于 抗病毒药物 疗法和诊断的设计。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于描述方案,并未报告其在临床或流行病学背景下的应用,这限制了其与公共卫生的直接相关性。

Expression and Purification of nsP2 Protease of Chikungunya Virus.

Authors: Barraza Sánchez JJ, Faraj SE
Original abstract (English)
The nonstructural protein 2 (nsP2) of chikungunya virus (CHIKV) is an essential cysteine protease involved in viral polyprotein processing and replication complex regulation. Biochemical and structural studies of nsP2 require the availability of highly pure and enzymatically active protein. In this chapter, we describe a straightforward protocol for the heterologous expression and purification of CHIKV nsP2 protease domain (nsP2pro) in Escherichia coli. The protein is expressed at low temperature as a His-tagged construct and purified under native conditions by immobilized metal ion affinity chromatography, followed by dialysis for buffer exchange. The procedure typically yields milligram quantities of highly pure (>95%) and active nsP2pro, and has been successfully applied to wild-type and mutant variants for enzymatic assays and inhibitor screening.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a method for expressing and purifying the nsP2 protease of Chikungunya virus, which is essential for viral replication and polyprotein processing.
Design and population
The study describes a protocol for the heterologous expression and purification of CHIKV nsP2 protease domain in Escherichia coli, focusing on the protein's production and isolation.
Core methods
The nsP2 protease domain was expressed at low temperature as a His-tagged construct and purified under native conditions using immobilized metal ion affinity chromatography, followed by dialysis for buffer exchange.
Main results
The procedure typically yields milligram quantities of highly pure (>95%) and active nsP2pro, and has been successfully applied to wild-type and mutant variants for enzymatic assays and inhibitor screening.
Interpretation and novelty
The described method provides a reliable and straightforward approach for obtaining active nsP2pro, which is valuable for further biochemical and structural studies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the development of tools for studying CHIKV protease function, which could aid in the design of antiviral therapies and diagnostics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to describing a protocol and does not report on its application in clinical or epidemiological contexts, which restricts its direct public health relevance.

基孔肯雅病毒 石蜡包埋组织中的感染:通过免疫组织化学分析组织学改变和病毒检测。

Chikungunya Virus Infection in Paraffin-Embedded Tissue: Analysis of Histological Alterations and Viral Detection by Immunohistochemistry.
作者： Salomão NG, Rabelo K
摘要中文翻译对 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 感染的组织(包括活检和尸检标本)进行组织病理学分析,可以为非典型和致命病例的 发病机制 提供有价值的见解。通过在显微镜下检查组织结构和细胞变化,可以识别损伤、炎症和细胞变性的模式。这些形态学发现提供了病原体如何与 宿主 细胞和组织相互作用的证据。此外,可以在组织切片中进行免疫组织化学以检测病毒抗原。重要的是,该技术可以表征组织样本中的多种生物学相关靶标,例如不同的免疫细胞子集、细胞因子和其他分子标记。这是通过使用针对目标抗原具有特异性而选择的抗体来实现的。在本章中,我们简要概述了组织学分析和免疫组织化学方法如何增强对 CHIKV 相关病理机制的理解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 感染组织的组织学改变和病毒检测,以更好地了解其在非典型和致命病例中的 发病机制。这对于识别与 CHIKV 感染相关的损伤、炎症和细胞变性模式非常重要。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 CHIKV 感染个体的活检和尸检标本的组织病理学变化,重点研究病毒如何与 宿主 细胞和组织相互作用。它包括一系列标本来捕捉不同的病理表现。
核心方法
这些方法涉及组织结构和细胞变化的组织学分析,以及检测病毒抗原的免疫组织化学。这些技术用于表征生物相关目标,例如组织样本中的免疫细胞亚群、细胞因子和分子标记。
主要结果
该研究观察到了受感染组织中 CHIKV 诱导的损伤、炎症和细胞变性的组织学改变。免疫组织化学证实这些样本中存在病毒抗原。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了组织学和免疫组织化学方法在阐明 CHIKV 感染病理机制方面的实用性。这为病毒与 宿主 组织的相互作用提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
结果可以为改进的诊断方法提供信息并增强对 CHIKV 发病机制 的理解,这对于开发有针对性的 监测 和 治疗 策略至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象级别的描述,不提供详细的统计或实验数据。由于缺乏具体的数值研究结果和综合分析,证据的强度中等。

Chikungunya Virus Infection in Paraffin-Embedded Tissue: Analysis of Histological Alterations and Viral Detection by Immunohistochemistry.

Authors: Salomão NG, Rabelo K
Original abstract (English)
Histopathological analysis of tissues infected with Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), including biopsy and autopsy specimens, can provide valuable insights into the pathogenesis of atypical and fatal cases. By examining tissue architecture and cellular alterations under the microscope, it is possible to identify patterns of injury, inflammation, and cellular degeneration. These morphological findings provide evidence of how the pathogen interacts with host cells and tissues. Moreover, immunohistochemistry may be performed in tissue sections for the detection of viral antigens. Importantly, this technique allows the characterization of diverse biologically relevant targets in tissue samples, such as distinct immune cell subsets, cytokines, and additional molecular markers. This is accomplished by the use of antibodies selected for their specificity toward the antigen of interest. In this chapter, we present a concise overview of how histological analysis and immunohistochemical approaches can enhance the understanding of CHIKV-associated pathological mechanisms.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the histological alterations and viral detection in tissues infected with Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) to better understand its pathogenesis in atypical and fatal cases. This is important for identifying patterns of injury, inflammation, and cellular degeneration associated with CHIKV infection.
Design and population
The study analyzed histopathological changes in biopsy and autopsy specimens from individuals with CHIKV infection, focusing on how the virus interacts with host cells and tissues. It included a range of specimens to capture diverse pathological manifestations.
Core methods
The methods involved histological analysis of tissue architecture and cellular alterations, along with immunohistochemistry to detect viral antigens. These techniques were used to characterize biologically relevant targets such as immune cell subsets, cytokines, and molecular markers in tissue samples.
Main results
The study observed histological alterations indicative of CHIKV-induced injury, inflammation, and cellular degeneration in infected tissues. Immunohistochemistry confirmed the presence of viral antigens in these samples.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the utility of histological and immunohistochemical approaches in elucidating the pathological mechanisms of CHIKV infection. This provides novel insights into the virus's interaction with host tissues.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform improved diagnostic methods and enhance understanding of CHIKV pathogenesis, which is critical for developing targeted surveillance and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level descriptions and does not provide detailed statistical or experimental data. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the lack of specific numerical findings and comprehensive analysis.

使用 acc-CHIK 15 -dnp 荧光肽底物评估 基孔肯雅病毒 蛋白酶活性和药理抑制。

Assessing Chikungunya Virus Protease Activity and Pharmacological Inhibition Using the acc-CHIK 15 -dnp Fluorogenic Peptide Substrate.
作者： Rahat MA, Makhaik S, Hardy JA
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒 是一种蚊媒甲病毒,由于其快速传播且缺乏经批准的 抗病毒药物 疗法,对全球健康构成重大威胁。病毒非结构蛋白 2 蛋白酶 (nsP2p) 在加工病毒多蛋白以产生关键病毒酶方面发挥着关键作用,使其成为药物开发的有吸引力的目标。在本研究中,我们描述了重组 CHIKV nsP2 蛋白酶的表达和纯化,并建立了强大的荧光测定法,使用 acc-CHIK 15 -dnp 肽底物测量其酶活性。该测定能够实时监测蛋白水解裂解,并提供评估小分子抑制剂的平台。我们提出了大肠杆菌中蛋白质表达、亲和纯化和标签去除的优化条件,从而获得适合生化表征的高纯度 nsP2 蛋白酶。使用该系统,我们还提供了一个强大的方案来确定候选抑制剂的 IC50 值。这项工作为 CHIKV 蛋白酶抑制剂的高通量筛选提供了一种可重复的方法,有助于发现针对 基孔肯雅病毒 的潜在 抗病毒药物 化合物。

Assessing Chikungunya Virus Protease Activity and Pharmacological Inhibition Using the acc-CHIK 15 -dnp Fluorogenic Peptide Substrate.

Authors: Rahat MA, Makhaik S, Hardy JA
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus, a mosquito-borne alphavirus, poses a significant global health threat due to its rapid spread and lack of approved antiviral therapies. The viral nonstructural protein 2 protease (nsP2p) plays a critical role in processing the viral polyprotein to produce key viral enzymes, making it an attractive target for drug development. In this study, we describe the expression and purification of recombinant CHIKV nsP2 protease and establish a robust fluorogenic assay to measure its enzymatic activity using the acc-CHIK 15 -dnp peptide substrate. The assay enables real-time monitoring of proteolytic cleavage and provides a platform for evaluating small-molecule inhibitors. We present optimized conditions for protein expression in E. coli, affinity purification, and tag removal, achieving high-purity nsP2 protease suitable for biochemical characterization. Using this system, we also provide a robust protocol for determining IC50 values for candidate inhibitors. This work provides a reproducible methodology for high-throughput screening of CHIKV protease inhibitors, facilitating the discovery of potential antiviral compounds against chikungunya virus.
View research seven-element analysis

Chikungunya virus, a mosquito-borne alphavirus, poses a significant global health threat due to its rapid spread and lack of approved antiviral therapies.
Design and population

Core methods
In this study, we describe the expression and purification of recombinant CHIKV nsP2 protease and establish a robust fluorogenic assay to measure its enzymatic activity using the acc-CHIK 15 -dnp peptide substrate.
Main results
Using this system, we also provide a robust protocol for determining IC50 values for candidate inhibitors.
Interpretation and novelty
This work provides a reproducible methodology for high-throughput screening of CHIKV protease inhibitors, facilitating the discovery of potential antiviral compounds against chikungunya virus.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of antiviral therapies for chikungunya virus, potentially reducing the global health burden.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not report limitations or evidence strength.

使用噬菌斑测定法测定 基孔肯雅病毒 感染滴度。

Determination of Chikungunya Virus Infectious Titer Using Plaque Assays.
作者： Volpe S, Filomatori C
摘要中文翻译空斑形成测定是测量感染性病毒颗粒的金标准方法。在该技术中,裂解病毒感染并破坏 宿主 细胞,但它们的传播受到粘性覆盖介质的限制。因此,新的病毒颗粒只能感染邻近的细胞,形成称为斑块的局部透明区域,在对剩余的活细胞进行染色后,斑块变得可见。噬斑数量反映了样品中最初存在的传染性病毒颗粒的数量,并以每样品体积的噬菌斑形成单位 (PFU) 报告。在此协议中,我们逐步描述如何进行噬菌斑形成测定以确定细胞培养上清液中 基孔肯雅病毒 的浓度。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在建立一种可靠的方法来定量细胞培养上清液中的感染性 基孔肯雅病毒 颗粒。
设计与对象
该研究描述了一种空斑形成测定方案,用于测量细胞培养上清液中的感染性病毒颗粒,但没有提及特定的群体或样本量。

主要结果
测定中形成的噬菌斑数量反映了样品中最初存在的感染性病毒颗粒的数量,并报告为每样品体积的噬菌斑形成单位(PFU)。
解释与创新
作者将空斑形成测定解释为量化感染性 基孔肯雅病毒 颗粒的直接而准确的方法,为病毒学分析提供了标准化方法。
科研与公卫意义

局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于描述该方法,没有报告具体结果,但证据很充分,因为它概述了测量传染性病毒颗粒的金标准技术。

Determination of Chikungunya Virus Infectious Titer Using Plaque Assays.

Authors: Volpe S, Filomatori C
Original abstract (English)
The plaque-forming assay is the gold-standard method for measuring infectious viral particles. In this technique, lytic viruses infect and destroy host cells, but their spread is restricted by a viscous overlay medium. As a result, new viral particles can only infect neighboring cells, leading to localized clear areas known as plaques, which become visible after staining the remaining living cells. The number of plaques reflects the number of infectious viral particles initially present in the sample and is reported as plaque-forming units (PFU) per sample volume. In this protocol, we describe step by step how to perform a plaque-forming assay to determine the concentration of chikungunya virus in a cell culture supernatant.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to establish a reliable method for quantifying infectious chikungunya virus particles in cell culture supernatants.
Design and population
The study describes a plaque-forming assay protocol to measure infectious viral particles in cell culture supernatants, with no specific population or sample size mentioned.
Core methods
The plaque-forming assay is described as the gold-standard method for measuring infectious viral particles, where lytic viruses infect and destroy host cells, but their spread is restricted by a viscous overlay medium.
Main results
The number of plaques formed in the assay reflects the number of infectious viral particles initially present in the sample and is reported as plaque-forming units (PFU) per sample volume.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the plaque-forming assay as a direct and accurate method for quantifying infectious chikungunya virus particles, providing a standardized approach for virological analysis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a reliable method for quantifying infectious chikungunya virus particles, which is essential for virological surveillance and understanding viral load dynamics in outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to describing the method without reporting specific results, but the evidence is strong as it outlines the gold-standard technique for measuring infectious viral particles.

基孔肯雅病毒 形成的斑块的自动计数。

Automated Counting of Chikungunya Virus-Formed Plaques.
作者： Emi A, Suzuki Y
摘要中文翻译细胞病变效应 (CPE) 是了解 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 致病性的关键,可用于评估体外病毒感染性。空斑测定基于病毒感染诱导的 CPE,为测试样本提供感染性病毒滴度。此外,该测定方法简单、定量且具有成本效益。因此,噬菌斑测定仍然是确定 CHIKV 感染性的金标准。然而,用肉眼或在显微镜下手动计数病毒诱导的斑块通常既耗时又费力。因此,自动噬菌斑计数软件将提高噬菌斑计数的一致性和效率。我们最近开发了 plaQuest,这是一款独立的 Windows 软件,可实现快速、可靠的 CHIKV 菌斑计数。在本章中,我们描述了在 24 孔板中检测和计数 CHIKV 形成的噬菌斑的基本程序。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决通过细胞病变效应 (CPE) 评估 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 致病性的挑战,这对于了解病毒体外感染性至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究描述了一种在 24 孔板中检测和计数 CHIKV 形成的噬菌斑的方法,重点关注用于噬菌斑测定的程序。
核心方法
空斑测定基于病毒感染引起的细胞病变效应,用于确定测试样品的感染性病毒滴度。该测定方法简单、定量且具有成本效益。
主要结果
空斑测定仍然是确定 CHIKV 感染性的金标准,因为它可以准确测量病毒滴度。
解释与创新
作者解释说,噬菌斑测定是评估 CHIKV 感染性的可靠方法,并强调其作为标准技术的持续相关性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持在 CHIKV 病毒学研究中继续使用噬斑测定,这对于了解其致病性和开发诊断工具至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于描述程序,不提供新的结果或比较。证据是温和的,因为它是基于既定方法及其优点。

Automated Counting of Chikungunya Virus-Formed Plaques.

Authors: Emi A, Suzuki Y
Original abstract (English)
The cytopathic effect (CPE) is a key to understanding the pathogenicity of the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) and is useful for evaluating viral infectivity in vitro. The plaque assay, based on the CPE induced by viral infection, provides an infectious virus titer for test samples. Additionally, the assay is simple, quantitative, and cost-effective. Thus, the plaque assay remains the gold standard for determining the infectivity of CHIKV. However, manually counting of virus-induced plaques with the naked eye or under the microscope is often time-consuming and labor-intensive. Therefore, automated plaque-counting software would improve the consistency and efficiency of plaque counting. We recently developed plaQuest, a stand-alone Windows software that enables rapid, reliable plaque counting for CHIKV. In this chapter, we describe the basic procedure for detecting and counting the CHIKV-formed plaques in a 24-well plate.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of evaluating the pathogenicity of the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) through the cytopathic effect (CPE), which is crucial for understanding viral infectivity in vitro.
Design and population
The study describes a method for detecting and counting CHIKV-formed plaques in a 24-well plate, focusing on the procedures used for plaque assay.
Core methods
The plaque assay, which is based on the cytopathic effect induced by viral infection, is used to determine the infectious virus titer for test samples. The assay is noted to be simple, quantitative, and cost-effective.
Main results
The plaque assay remains the gold standard for determining the infectivity of CHIKV, as it provides an accurate measure of viral titer.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the plaque assay is a reliable method for assessing CHIKV infectivity, and they highlight its continued relevance as a standard technique.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the continued use of plaque assays in virology research for CHIKV, which is essential for understanding its pathogenicity and developing diagnostic tools.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to describing the procedure and does not provide new results or comparisons. The evidence is moderate as it is based on established methods and their advantages.

对易感社区中埃及伊蚊分布和基孔肯雅热风险的社会环境评估。

Socio-environmental assessment of Aedes aegypti distribution and risk of chikungunya in vulnerable community.
作者： Jaqueline S Cruz, Hernan D Argibay, Nivison Nery, Hammed Mogaji, Thiago da M Barreto, Lorena Ellen S Batista, Lara R S Silva, Victor Kaua Nery, Victoria C Dedavid Ferreira, Ianei de O Carneiro
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)通过埃及伊蚊传播,在热带地区构成重大的公共卫生挑战。在巴西,其传播范围不断扩大,尤其是在社会经济脆弱地区,环境条件有利于媒介的繁殖。我们的目标是评估与Aedes aegypti空间分布以及居民基孔肯雅热暴露相关的社会经济和环境脆弱性。通过识别关键暴露热点,我们的工作提高了我们对基孔肯雅热在非正式聚居区传播机制的理解,并有助于制定基于社区的干预措施以减轻基孔肯雅热风险。在巴西萨尔瓦多的一个易感非正式社区中,使用卵笼捕捉埃及伊蚊,并于2021年10月至2022年6月期间对年龄≥5岁的居民进行了血清学调查。收集了居民的血液样本以及社会人口学、家庭和环境数据。进行了基孔肯雅病毒的血清学检测,并使用集成嵌套拉普拉斯近似法(Integrated Nested Laplace Approximation)的空间模型评估了媒介的分布、血清阳性率及相关因素。我们的高分辨率研究识别了与埃及伊蚊数量和基孔肯雅热传播风险相关的微尺度脆弱性。基于我们的研究结果,有效的控制策略不仅应关注传统的增强基本城市基础设施和服务以及促进社区意识的方法,还应针对家庭周围的特定繁殖场所,解决垃圾堆积问题,并考虑微尺度温度变化的影响。这些结果强调了针对高风险社区制定特定于社区的干预措施的重要性,以有效减少基孔肯雅热的传播。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)通过埃及伊蚊传播,在热带地区构成重大的公共卫生挑战。在巴西,其传播范围不断扩大,尤其是在社会经济脆弱地区,环境条件有利于媒介的繁殖。
设计与对象
我们的目标是评估与Aedes aegypti空间分布以及居民基孔肯雅热暴露相关的社会经济和环境脆弱性,在巴西萨尔瓦多的一个低收入社区中进行。
核心方法
从年龄≥5岁的居民中收集了血液样本以及社会人口学、家庭和环境数据。进行了基孔肯雅病毒的血清学检测,并使用集成嵌套拉普拉斯近似法(Integrated Nested Laplace Approximation)的空间模型评估了媒介的分布、血清阳性率及相关因素。
主要结果
我们的高分辨率研究识别了与埃及伊蚊数量和基孔肯雅热传播风险相关的微尺度脆弱性。
解释与创新
通过识别关键暴露热点,我们的工作提高了我们对基孔肯雅热在非正式聚居区传播机制的理解,并有助于制定基于社区的干预措施以减轻基孔肯雅热风险。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果强调了针对高风险社区制定特定于社区的干预措施的重要性,以有效减少基孔肯雅热的传播。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其仅关注单一社区,可能无法推广到其他环境。此外,使用血清学数据可能无法完全捕捉近期感染情况,且本研究未包括基孔肯雅病毒的分子检测。

Socio-environmental assessment of Aedes aegypti distribution and risk of chikungunya in vulnerable community.

Authors: Jaqueline S Cruz, Hernan D Argibay, Nivison Nery, Hammed Mogaji, Thiago da M Barreto, Lorena Ellen S Batista, Lara R S Silva, Victor Kaua Nery, Victoria C Dedavid Ferreira, Ianei de O Carneiro
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), transmitted by Aedes mosquitoes, poses a major public health challenge in tropical regions. In Brazil, its incidence has increased, especially in socioeconomically vulnerable areas, where environmental conditions favor the proliferation of vectors. Our objective was to assess the socioeconomic and environmental vulnerabilities associated with the spatial distribution of a) Ae. aegypti and b) resident exposure to CHIKV in a low-income community. Identifying key exposure hotspots, our work improves our understanding of CHIKV transmission in informal settlements and facilitates community-based interventions to mitigate CHIKV risk. aegypti using ovitraps and conducted a serological survey from October 2021 to June 2022 in a vulnerable informal community in Salvador, Brazil. Blood samples and sociodemographic, household, and environmental data were collected from residents aged ≥5 years. CHIKV serology was performed, and spatial models using Integrated Nested Laplace Approximation assessed vector distribution, seropositivity, and associated factors. Our high-resolution study identified fine-scale vulnerabilities linked to both Ae. aegypti abundance and heightened risk of CHIKV transmission. Based on our findings, effective control strategies should not only focus on traditional methods such as enhancing basic urban infrastructure and services and promoting community awareness, but also target specific breeding sites around homes, address garbage accumulation, and consider the influence of micro-scale temperature variations. These results emphasize the importance of tailored, neighborhood-specific interventions to effectively reduce chikungunya transmission in high-risk communities.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), transmitted by Aedes mosquitoes, poses a major public health challenge in tropical regions. In Brazil, its incidence has increased, especially in socioeconomically vulnerable areas, where environmental conditions favor the proliferation of vectors.
Design and population
Our objective was to assess the socioeconomic and environmental vulnerabilities associated with the spatial distribution of Aedes aegypti and resident exposure to CHIKV in a low-income community in Salvador, Brazil.
Core methods
Blood samples and sociodemographic, household, and environmental data were collected from residents aged ≥5 years. CHIKV serology was performed, and spatial models using Integrated Nested Laplace Approximation assessed vector distribution, seropositivity, and associated factors.
Main results
Our high-resolution study identified fine-scale vulnerabilities linked to both Aedes aegypti abundance and heightened risk of CHIKV transmission.
Interpretation and novelty
Identifying key exposure hotspots, our work improves our understanding of CHIKV transmission in informal settlements and facilitates community-based interventions to mitigate CHIKV risk.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results emphasize the importance of tailored, neighborhood-specific interventions to effectively reduce chikungunya transmission in high-risk communities.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single community, which may not be generalizable to other settings. Additionally, the use of serological data may not fully capture recent infections, and the study did not include molecular surveillance of CHIKV.

长期储存对纯化基孔肯雅病毒形态和感染力的影响

Effect of long-term storage on the morphology and infectivity of purified Chikungunya virus
作者： Licya Xavier, Marta Perez-Illana, Elisa Azevedo, Veronica Silva, Tayna Lima, Bruno Leite, Isabelle Viana, Roberto Lins
摘要中文翻译摘要 病毒颗粒的纯化和长期保存是病毒学研究中的关键步骤,因为它们直接影响支持疫苗和治疗开发的结构研究和功能实验的可靠性。许多这些应用,特别是冷冻电子显微镜(cryo-EM)和生物物理分析,需要无冷冻保护剂(CPA-free)的病毒制备,但在这种条件下保持感染力和结构完整性对包膜病毒来说尤为具有挑战性。在这里,我们描述并验证了一种实用的方法,用于纯化和保存不使用冷冻保护剂的感染性基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)颗粒。从Vero细胞中生产的CHIKV颗粒通过聚乙二醇(PEG)沉淀和不连续铯氯化物(CsCl)梯度超速离心进行纯化,获得了高纯度的制备,回收率为70.4%,并且病毒颗粒形态得以保留,这通过SDS-PAGE、RT-qPCR和负染色传播电子显微镜(TEM)确认。为了确定冷冻速率和超低温储存条件对长期保存的影响,纯化的CHIKV被置于三种无冷冻保护剂的冷冻/储存方案中:快速冷冻并储存在液氮中、快速冷冻后储存在-80°C、以及控制缓慢冷冻后储存在-80°C。感染力通过冻后202天内的斑块实验进行监测。尽管感染力随时间逐渐下降,但在所测试的方案中未观察到显著差异,这表明在-80°C下进行控制缓慢冷冻是一种实用且成本效益高的替代液氮储存的方法。TEM显示在所有储存条件下病毒颗粒的整体形态均得以保留,尽管感染力有所下降,这表明细微的结构变化可能无法通过常规电子显微镜检测到。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决在不使用冷冻保护剂的情况下保持基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)颗粒的感染力和结构完整性的挑战,因为冷冻保护剂对于支持疫苗和治疗开发的可靠结构研究和功能实验至关重要。
设计与对象
本研究涉及从Vero细胞中纯化CHIKV颗粒,并将其置于三种无冷冻保护剂的冷冻/储存方案中,以评估其在不同条件下的长期保存效果。
核心方法
CHIKV颗粒通过聚乙二醇(PEG)沉淀和不连续铯氯化物(CsCl)梯度超速离心进行纯化。感染力通过斑块实验进行评估,病毒颗粒形态则通过负染色传播电子显微镜(TEM)进行分析。
主要结果
纯化的CHIKV颗粒在所有储存条件下均表现出70.4%的回收率,并且病毒颗粒形态得以保留。然而,感染力随时间逐渐下降,且在所测试的方案中未观察到显著差异。
解释与创新
作者认为,在-80°C下进行控制缓慢冷冻是一种实用且成本效益高的替代液氮储存方法,用于在不使用冷冻保护剂的情况下保存CHIKV感染力,因为在所测试的方案中未发现保存效果的显著差异。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为在不使用冷冻保护剂的情况下保存CHIKV颗粒提供了可靠的方法,从而支持了结构和功能研究,进而有助于疫苗和治疗开发。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅基于摘要证据,且结果基于单一方案的比较。证据强度中等,因为这些发现基于观察到的感染力下降和形态保存,而没有长期随访或额外验证。

Effect of long-term storage on the morphology and infectivity of purified Chikungunya virus

Authors: Licya Xavier, Marta Perez-Illana, Elisa Azevedo, Veronica Silva, Tayna Lima, Bruno Leite, Isabelle Viana, Roberto Lins
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Purification and long-term preservation of viral particles are critical steps in virology research, as they directly influence the reliability of structural studies and functional assays supporting vaccine and therapeutic development. Many of these applications, particularly cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) and biophysical analyses, require cryoprotectant-free (CPA-free) viral preparations, yet preserving both infectivity and structural integrity under such conditions is especially challenging for enveloped viruses. Here, we describe and validate a practical methodology for purifying and preserving infectious Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) particles for applications in which cryoprotectants cannot be used. CHIKV particles produced in Vero cells were purified by polyethylene glycol (PEG) precipitation followed by discontinuous cesium chloride (CsCl) gradient ultracentrifugation, yielding highly purified preparations with a 70.4% recovery rate and preserved virion morphology, as confirmed by SDS-PAGE, RT-qPCR and negative-staining transmission electron microscopy (TEM). To determine whether freezing rate and ultra-low storage temperature affect long-term preservation, purified CHIKV was subjected to three CPA-free freezing/storage protocols: fast freezing and storage in liquid nitrogen, fast freezing followed by storage at -80°C, and controlled slow freezing followed by storage at -80°C. Infectivity was monitored by plaque assay for up to 202 days post-freezing. Although infectivity declined progressively, no significant differences were observed among the protocols tested, indicating that controlled slow freezing at -80°C is a practical and cost-effective alternative to liquid nitrogen storage. TEM showed preservation of the overall virion morphology under all storage conditions despite the reduced infectivity, suggesting that subtle structural alterations may escape detection by conventional electron microscopy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of preserving the infectivity and structural integrity of Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) particles without the use of cryoprotectants, which are critical for reliable structural studies and functional assays in vaccine and therapeutic development.
Design and population
The study involved purifying CHIKV particles from Vero cells and subjecting them to three CPA-free freezing/storage protocols to evaluate their long-term preservation under different conditions.
Core methods
CHIKV particles were purified using polyethylene glycol (PEG) precipitation and discontinuous cesium chloride (CsCl) gradient ultracentrifugation. Infectivity was assessed using plaque assay, and virion morphology was evaluated by negative-staining transmission electron microscopy (TEM).
Main results
Purified CHIKV particles showed a 70.4% recovery rate and preserved virion morphology across all storage conditions. However, infectivity declined progressively over time, with no significant differences observed among the tested protocols.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that controlled slow freezing at -80°C is a practical and cost-effective alternative to liquid nitrogen storage for preserving CHIKV infectivity without cryoprotectants, as no significant differences in preservation were found among the protocols.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a reliable method for preserving CHIKV particles for structural and functional studies, which can support vaccine and therapeutic development without the need for cryoprotectants.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results were based on a single protocol comparison. The evidence is moderate, as the findings are based on observed infectivity decline and morphology preservation without long-term follow-up or additional validation.

意大利帕勒莫大学护理专业学生的基孔肯雅热认知及相关知识、态度和行为:一项关于相关社会人口学因素的横断面研究

Chikungunya Awareness and Related Knowledge, Attitudes, and Practices Among Nursing Students at the University of Palermo, Italy: A Cross-Sectional Study of Associated Sociodemographic Factors
作者： Omar Enzo Santangelo, Sandro Provenzano, Luana Maria Amato, Simona Fanuli, Anna Sole Pizzamiglio, Carlotta Vella, Giuseppina Pesco, Giuseppe D’Anna, Alberto Firenze, Antonio Terranova
摘要中文翻译引言:基孔肯雅热是一种新兴的蚊媒病毒性疾病,已成为全球日益严重的公共卫生问题。尽管在意大利已记录到本地传播的基孔肯雅病毒,且欧洲范围内白纹伊蚊的传播范围不断扩大,但关于未来医疗专业人员对基孔肯雅热的认知仍知之甚少。本研究旨在评估护理专业学生对基孔肯雅热的认知,并评估其相关知识、态度和行为(KAPs)。方法:2026年5月,在意大利帕勒莫大学进行了一项横断面研究,调查在读护理专业本科生。通过匿名的自我管理在线问卷收集数据,内容涉及社会人口学特征、基孔肯雅热的认知、信息来源以及与该疾病相关的KAP。使用卡方检验和比值比分析评估认知与参与者特征之间的关联。结果:共有894名学生参与(平均年龄:22.33 ± 4.53岁;72.3%为女性)。总体而言,仅有22名学生(2.46%)表示之前听说过基孔肯雅热。在多变量探索性分析中,第三年学生比第一年学生有更高的基孔肯雅热认知比值比(PR = 3.38;95% CI:1.10–10.36),而年龄与认知之间未观察到统计学显著关联(PR = 1.07;95% CI:1.00–1.15)。大众媒体是主要的信息来源(63.6%),其次是大学课程(22.7%)。在报告过基孔肯雅热认知的参与者中,其对主要临床表现的识别相对较好,但关于基孔肯雅病毒传播途径的识别仍存在重要差距。参与者对预防的态度总体积极,大多数参与者报告采用了推荐的蚊虫控制措施。结论:护理专业学生对基孔肯雅热的认知极低。在护理课程中加强关于新兴蚊媒病毒的教育干预,可能有助于提高应对能力、风险沟通能力和未来的公共卫生响应水平。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基孔肯雅热是一种新兴的蚊媒病毒性疾病,已成为全球日益严重的公共卫生问题。尽管在意大利已记录到本地传播的基孔肯雅病毒,且欧洲范围内白纹伊蚊的传播范围不断扩大,但关于未来医疗专业人员对基孔肯雅热的认知仍知之甚少。
设计与对象
2026 年 5 月,对巴勒莫大学注册的护理本科生进行了一项横断面研究。共有 894 名学生参与(平均年龄:22.33 ± 4.53 岁;72.3% 为女性)。
核心方法
通过匿名自填在线调查问卷收集数据,调查社会人口学特征、基孔肯雅热认知度、信息来源以及与该疾病相关的 KAP。使用卡方检验和 患病率 比率分析来评估意识和参与者特征之间的关联。
主要结果
总体而言,只有 22 名学生 (2.46%) 表示以前听说过基孔肯雅热。在多变量探索性分析中,与一年级学生相比,三年级学生的 患病率 先前认知度更高(PR = 3.38;95% CI:1.10–10.36),而与年龄没有观察到统计学上显着的关联(PR = 1.07;95% CI:1.00–1.15)。大众媒体是主要的信息来源(63.6%),其次是大学讲座(22.7%)。
解释与创新
护生对基孔肯雅热的认知度极低。在报告先前意识的参与者中,反应表明对主要临床表现的识别相对较好,尽管在 媒介 识别和 传播 通路方面观察到重要差距。
科研与公卫意义
在护理课程中加强对新出现的 媒介 传播疾病的教育干预可能会改善准备情况、风险沟通和未来的公共卫生应对措施。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告本研究的局限性。

Chikungunya Awareness and Related Knowledge, Attitudes, and Practices Among Nursing Students at the University of Palermo, Italy: A Cross-Sectional Study of Associated Sociodemographic Factors

Authors: Omar Enzo Santangelo, Sandro Provenzano, Luana Maria Amato, Simona Fanuli, Anna Sole Pizzamiglio, Carlotta Vella, Giuseppina Pesco, Giuseppe D’Anna, Alberto Firenze, Antonio Terranova
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Chikungunya is an emerging mosquito-borne viral disease that has become a growing public health concern worldwide. Despite documented autochthonous outbreaks in Italy and the increasing spread of Aedes albopictus mosquitoes across Europe, little is known about awareness of chikungunya among future healthcare professionals. This study aimed to assess awareness of chikungunya and evaluate related knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAPs) among nursing students at the University of Palermo, Italy. Methods: A cross-sectional study was conducted in May 2026 among undergraduate nursing students enrolled at the University of Palermo. Data were collected through an anonymous self-administered online questionnaire investigating sociodemographic characteristics, awareness of chikungunya, sources of information, and KAP related to the disease. Associations between awareness and participant characteristics were assessed using chi-square tests and prevalence ratio analyses. Results: A total of 894 students participated (mean age: 22.33 ± 4.53 years; 72.3% female). Overall, only 22 students (2.46%) reported having previously heard of chikungunya. In a multivariable exploratory analysis, third-year students had a higher prevalence of previous awareness compared with first-year students (PR = 3.38; 95% CI: 1.10–10.36), whereas no statistically significant association was observed for age (PR = 1.07; 95% CI: 1.00–1.15). Mass media represented the main source of information (63.6%), followed by university lectures (22.7%). Among participants who reported previous awareness, responses suggested relatively good recognition of major clinical manifestations, although important gaps were observed regarding vector recognition and transmission pathways. Attitudes toward prevention were predominantly positive, and most participants reported adopting recommended mosquito-control practices. Conclusions: Awareness of chikungunya among nursing students was extremely low. Strengthening educational interventions on emerging vector-borne diseases within nursing curricula may improve preparedness, risk communication, and future public health responses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chikungunya is an emerging mosquito-borne viral disease that has become a growing public health concern worldwide. Despite documented autochthonous outbreaks in Italy and the increasing spread of Aedes albopictus mosquitoes across Europe, little is known about awareness of chikungunya among future healthcare professionals.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted in May 2026 among undergraduate nursing students enrolled at the University of Palermo. A total of 894 students participated (mean age: 22.33 ± 4.53 years; 72.3% female).
Core methods
Data were collected through an anonymous self-administered online questionnaire investigating sociodemographic characteristics, awareness of chikungunya, sources of information, and KAP related to the disease. Associations between awareness and participant characteristics were assessed using chi-square tests and prevalence ratio analyses.
Main results
Overall, only 22 students (2.46%) reported having previously heard of chikungunya. In a multivariable exploratory analysis, third-year students had a higher prevalence of previous awareness compared with first-year students (PR = 3.38; 95% CI: 1.10–10.36), whereas no statistically significant association was observed for age (PR = 1.07; 95% CI: 1.00–1.15). Mass media represented the main source of information (63.6%), followed by university lectures (22.7%).
Interpretation and novelty
Awareness of chikungunya among nursing students was extremely low. Among participants who reported previous awareness, responses suggested relatively good recognition of major clinical manifestations, although important gaps were observed regarding vector recognition and transmission pathways.
Scientific and public-health significance
Strengthening educational interventions on emerging vector-borne diseases within nursing curricula may improve preparedness, risk communication, and future public health responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.

甲病毒 Nsp3 蛋白通过 BNIP3L/NIX 劫持受体介导的线粒体自噬,抑制 IFN 反应并促进病毒复制

Alphavirus Nsp3 protein hijacks receptor-mediated mitophagy via BNIP3L/NIX to dampen IFN response and promote viral replication
作者： Xiao-Yi Qi, Yuan Zhao, Zhen-Bang Zhu, Wei Wen, Wen-Qiang Wang, Xiang-Dong Li
摘要中文翻译甲病毒包括多种人类和动物病原体,例如盖塔病毒(GETV)、基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)和辛德比斯病毒(SINV)。越来越多的证据表明,先天免疫在宿主抵抗甲病毒感染的过程中发挥着重要作用,而甲病毒已经进化出多种策略来逃避宿主 宿主先天免疫。线粒体作为关键的免疫中枢,在先天 抗病毒药物 反应的启动中发挥着核心作用。目前尚不清楚甲病毒是否通过操纵线粒体来抑制先天 免疫应答。在这项研究中,我们发现 GETV 感染会损害线粒体的结构和功能,表现为线粒体膜电位去极化、线粒体数量减少和线粒体动力学失衡。GETV 感染诱导 宿主 细胞中的线粒体自噬并促进病毒复制。甲病毒(CHIKV、SINV、GETV)的 Nsp3 蛋白可以通过与 BNIP3L/NIX 相互作用直接触发受体介导的线粒体自噬。敲除 BNIP3L 可以减轻 GETV 感染引起的线粒体自噬。甲病毒 Nsp3 蛋白诱导的线粒体自噬导致 MAVS 降解,从而抑制 IFN 反应并促进病毒复制。总的来说,我们的结果表明甲病毒 Nsp3 作为一种新型毒力因子,诱导 BNIP3L 介导的线粒体自噬来降解 MAVS 并逃避 宿主 免疫应答。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查甲病毒是否通过操纵线粒体来抑制先天性 免疫应答,因为目前尚不清楚这些病毒如何与 宿主 免疫机制相互作用。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注甲病毒,特别是 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV),并在体外和体内检查了它们对 宿主 细胞线粒体和 免疫应答 的影响。
核心方法
研究人员使用分子和细胞技术来评估线粒体结构和功能,包括测量线粒体膜电位、线粒体数量和动力学。他们还评估了线粒体自噬及其对 MAVS 降解和 IFN 反应的影响。
主要结果
研究发现,CHIKV 感染会损害线粒体的结构和功能,表现为线粒体膜电位去极化、线粒体数量减少和线粒体动力学失衡。此外,CHIKV 感染会诱导线粒体自噬,从而促进病毒复制。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为甲病毒(包括 CHIKV)的 Nsp3 蛋白诱导 BNIP3L 介导的线粒体自噬以降解 MAVS 的证据,从而抑制 IFN 反应并促进病毒复制。这代表了甲病毒逃避 宿主 免疫的新机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了对 CHIKV 逃避 宿主 免疫的分子机制的见解,这可以为未来 抗病毒药物 策略的研究提供信息,并提高对 CHIKV 发病机制 的理解。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外和体内模型,证据仅基于抽象数据。然而,这些结果得到了直接观察和分子分析的支持,表明具有中等的置信度。

Alphavirus Nsp3 protein hijacks receptor-mediated mitophagy via BNIP3L/NIX to dampen IFN response and promote viral replication

Authors: Xiao-Yi Qi, Yuan Zhao, Zhen-Bang Zhu, Wei Wen, Wen-Qiang Wang, Xiang-Dong Li
Original abstract (English)
Alphaviruses encompass a variety of human and animal pathogens, such as getah virus (GETV), chikungunya virus (CHIKV), and Sindbis virus (SINV). Increasing evidence suggests that innate immunity plays an important role in host resistance to alphavirus infection, while alphaviruses have evolved multiple strategies to evade host antiviral innate immunity. Mitochondria, as key immune hubs, play a central role in the initiation of innate antiviral responses. It remains unclear whether alphaviruses inhibit innate immune responses by manipulating mitochondria. In this study, we found that GETV infection damages mitochondrial structure and function, as shown by mitochondrial membrane potential depolarization, reduction in mitochondrial numbers, and an imbalance in mitochondrial dynamics. GETV infection induces mitophagy in host cells and promotes viral replication. Nsp3 protein of alphaviruses (CHIKV, SINV, GETV) can directly trigger receptor-mediated mitophagy through its interaction with BNIP3L/NIX. Knockdown of BNIP3L could alleviate mitophagy caused by GETV infection. Mitophagy induced by alphavirus Nsp3 protein leads to degradation of MAVS which inhibits the IFN response and promotes viral replication. Collectively, our results demonstrated that alphaviruses Nsp3 serves as a novel virulence factor, inducing BNIP3L-mediated mitophagy to degrade MAVS and evade the host immune response.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate whether alphaviruses inhibit innate immune responses by manipulating mitochondria, as it remains unclear how these viruses interact with host immune mechanisms.
Design and population
The study focused on alphaviruses, specifically Chikungunya virus (CHIKV), and examined their effects on host cell mitochondria and immune responses in vitro and in vivo.
Core methods
The researchers used molecular and cellular techniques to assess mitochondrial structure and function, including measuring mitochondrial membrane potential, mitochondrial numbers, and dynamics. They also evaluated mitophagy and its impact on MAVS degradation and IFN response.
Main results
The study found that CHIKV infection damages mitochondrial structure and function, as evidenced by mitochondrial membrane potential depolarization, reduction in mitochondrial numbers, and an imbalance in mitochondrial dynamics. Additionally, CHIKV infection induces mitophagy, which promotes viral replication.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence that the Nsp3 protein of alphaviruses, including CHIKV, induces BNIP3L-mediated mitophagy to degrade MAVS, thereby inhibiting the IFN response and promoting viral replication. This represents a novel mechanism by which alphaviruses evade host immunity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide insights into the molecular mechanisms by which CHIKV evades host immunity, which could inform future research on antiviral strategies and improve understanding of CHIKV pathogenesis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and in vivo models, and the evidence is based on abstract-only data. However, the results are supported by direct observations and molecular analyses, suggesting a moderate level of confidence.

间日疟原虫和基孔肯雅热混合感染;当发热性疾病演变为高动力休克时:病例报告

COINFECTION WITH PLASMODIUM VIVAX AND CHIKUNGUNYA; WHEN FEBRILE ILLNESS EVOLVES INTO HYPERDYNAMIC SHOCK: CASE REPORT
作者： Jexon Noriel Guido Treminio, Rainier Sanders Manzanares, Joselyn María Ríos Müller, Antonia Margarita Gutiérrez Mendoza, Andrés Uriel Rivas Duarte, Kelvin Hernaldo Mercado Poveda, Joseph Lynn Hansack Ignacio, John Anthony Cajina Rodríguez, Toshiro Eduardo Mejía Chang, Danny Taylor Watler
摘要中文翻译疟疾和基孔肯雅热的混合感染是一种罕见的表现,对诊断提出了挑战,特别是因为两种感染都会产生全身表现和血流动力学损害。我们介绍了一名来自城市地区的 23 岁女性患者,无既往病史,有 7 天发热、头晕、全身无力、关节痛 和呕吐病史。入院时,她出现发热、心动过速和低血压,对晶体液初始复苏的血流动力学反应不足,需要血管加压药支持。高级心血管评估显示主要是血管舒张和高动力特征,其特征是高心输出量、降低的全身血管阻力和保留的每搏输出量。左心室和右心室收缩和舒张功能仍然保留,具有足够的 TAPSE 和 MAPSE,并且没有明显的心室扩张或局部收缩力异常。心电图和心肌酶显示没有心肌损伤或心肌炎的证据。研究显示严重贫血,血红蛋白水平为 6 g/dL,并且肝脾肿大。厚厚的血涂片鉴定出疟原虫。该患者的间日疟检测呈阳性,而基孔肯雅热PCR检测呈阳性,登革热检测呈阴性,证实了双重感染。开始静脉注射青蒿琥酯,随后使用蒿甲醚-本芴醇,此外还进行血管活性支持和输注两个单位的浓缩红细胞。患者的血流动力学逐渐恢复,临床病程良好。该病例强调了考虑 媒介 传播的合并感染和表征血流动力学表型以区分血管舒张性休克和心源性损害的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在强调间日疟原虫和 基孔肯雅病毒 合并感染带来的诊断挑战,因为两者都会产生全身表现和血流动力学损害。
设计与对象
病例报告 对一名来自城市地区的 23 岁女性患者进行,该患者无既往病史,表现为发烧、头晕、全身无力、病例报告 和呕吐。
核心方法
通过厚血涂片和PCR检测确认了患者的诊断,分别鉴定出间日疟原虫和基孔肯雅病毒,同时排除了登革热病毒。
主要结果
高级心血管评估显示出高动力特征,心输出量高,全身血管阻力降低,每搏输出量保持不变。心电图和心肌酶显示没有心肌损伤或心肌炎的证据。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,间日疟原虫和 基孔肯雅病毒 的共感染可导致独特的血流动力学表型,因此需要与其他休克原因仔细区分。
科研与公卫意义
该病例强调了在临床环境中识别 媒介 传播的合并感染的重要性,并强调需要准确的血流动力学特征来指导适当的治疗。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个病例报告,可能无法推广。然而,鉴于详细的临床和诊断结果,证据是强有力的。

COINFECTION WITH PLASMODIUM VIVAX AND CHIKUNGUNYA; WHEN FEBRILE ILLNESS EVOLVES INTO HYPERDYNAMIC SHOCK: CASE REPORT

Authors: Jexon Noriel Guido Treminio, Rainier Sanders Manzanares, Joselyn María Ríos Müller, Antonia Margarita Gutiérrez Mendoza, Andrés Uriel Rivas Duarte, Kelvin Hernaldo Mercado Poveda, Joseph Lynn Hansack Ignacio, John Anthony Cajina Rodríguez, Toshiro Eduardo Mejía Chang, Danny Taylor Watler
Original abstract (English)
Coinfection with malaria and chikungunya is a rare presentation and poses a diagnostic challenge., especially since both infections can produce systemic manifestations and hemodynamic compromise. We present the case of a 23-year-old female patient from an urban area with no known past medical history, who presented with a seven-day history of fever, dizziness, generalized weakness, arthralgia, and vomiting. On admission, she was febrile, tachycardic, and hypotensive, with an insufficient hemodynamic response to initial resuscitation with crystalloids, requiring vasopressor support. Advanced cardiovascular evaluation revealed a predominantly vasodilatory and hyperdynamic profile, characterized by high cardiac output, decreased systemic vascular resistance, and preserved stroke volume. Left and right ventricular systolic and diastolic function remained preserved, with adequate TAPSE and MAPSE, and no significant chamber dilation or regional contractility abnormalities. The electrocardiogram and cardiac enzymes showed no evidence of myocardial injury or myocarditis. Studies revealed severe anemia, with a hemoglobin level of 6 g/ dL, and hepatosplenomegaly. Thick blood smear identified Plasmodium. The patient tested positive for vivax, while the PCR test for chikungunya was positive and the dengue test was negative, confirming the coinfection. Intravenous artesunate was initiated, followed by artemether-lumefantrine, in addition to vasoactive support and transfusion of two units of packed red blood cells. The patient experienced progressive hemodynamic recovery and a favorable clinical course. This case highlights the importance of considering vector-borne coinfections and characterizing the hemodynamic phenotype to differentiate vasodilatory shock from cardiogenic compromise.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to highlight the diagnostic challenges posed by coinfection with Plasmodium vivax and Chikungunya virus, as both can produce systemic manifestations and hemodynamic compromise.
Design and population
A case report was conducted on a 23-year-old female patient from an urban area with no known past medical history, who presented with fever, dizziness, generalized weakness, arthralgia, and vomiting.
Core methods
The patient's diagnosis was confirmed through thick blood smear and PCR testing, which identified Plasmodium vivax and Chikungunya virus, respectively, while ruling out dengue virus.
Main results
Advanced cardiovascular evaluation revealed a hyperdynamic profile with high cardiac output, decreased systemic vascular resistance, and preserved stroke volume. The electrocardiogram and cardiac enzymes showed no evidence of myocardial injury or myocarditis.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that coinfection with Plasmodium vivax and Chikungunya virus can lead to a distinct hemodynamic phenotype, necessitating careful differentiation from other causes of shock.
Scientific and public-health significance
This case underscores the importance of recognizing vector-borne coinfections in clinical settings and emphasizes the need for accurate hemodynamic characterization to guide appropriate management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single case report, which may not be generalizable. However, the evidence is strong given the detailed clinical and diagnostic findings.

针对基孔肯雅病毒 nsP2pro的潜在抑制剂计算识别

Computational identification of potential inhibitors for chikungunya virus nsP2pro
作者： Nguyen H, Ngo S
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在确定 基孔肯雅病毒 nsP2pro 的潜在抑制剂,以解决 基孔肯雅病毒 感染缺乏批准疗法的问题。
设计与对象
使用美国国家癌症研究所 (NCI) 数据库进行了一项计算研究,以筛选可能的 nsP2pro 抑制剂,重点是 基孔肯雅病毒 及其非结构蛋白 2 蛋白酶。
核心方法
采用包括使用 AutoDock Vina 进行分子对接模拟、使用 GROMACS 进行分子动力学模拟以及自由能微扰 (FEP) 模拟等计算方法来评估配体结合亲和力和构象变化。
主要结果
五种化合物,NSC 319990、NSC 80731、NSC 80735、NSC 67436和NSC 37553,表现出显著更强的结合亲和力,范围从–9.70 ± 0.61到–11.71 ± 1.25 kcal mol–1,与现有抑制剂RA-0002034相比(其ΔGFEP为−6.45 ± 0.32 kcal mol–1)。当这些抑制剂存在时,Cys478-Sγ–Nε-His548对的距离显著增大。
解释与创新
作者解释说,这五种化合物更强的结合亲和力与其抑制 nsP2pro 生物活性的能力相关,表明它们是有希望进一步实验验证的候选化合物。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为针对 nsP2pro 的新型治疗策略的开发提供信息,从而有可能导致针对 基孔肯雅病毒 感染的新 治疗。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到其计算性质的限制,可能无法完全捕捉生物的复杂性。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它基于严格的模拟和统计验证。

Computational identification of potential inhibitors for chikungunya virus nsP2pro

Authors: Nguyen H, Ngo S
Original abstract (English)
In this study, possible inhibitors for nsP2pro from the National Cancer Institute (NCI) database were screened via computational approaches. Overall, these findings suggest that five NCI compounds are highly promising candidates for further experimental validation as novel nsP2pro inhibitors.Keywords: chikungunya virus, non-structural protein 2 protease, docking, free energy perturbation, molecular dynamics simulation 2.Material and methods2.1.Chikungunya virus non-structural protein 2 protease and ligand conformationsThe three-dimensional structure of CHIKV nsP2pro was downloaded from the Protein Data Bank (PDB) with PDB ID: 4ZTB) [8]. The number of atoms of the complex nsP2pro + inhibitor and the free inhibitor are ca 76 000 and 3660 atoms.Both systems were initially minimized using the steepest descent method. This approach is called the double-annihilation binding free energy method [50,51]. Supplementary Material Supplementary Materials 3.Results and discussion3.1.Docking affinity and poseAs mentioned above, the CHIKV nsP2pro is one of the most favoured targets for drug design because it is associated with the viral replication [6]. MD simulations were then performed to refine the ligand binding pose. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to identify potential inhibitors for the chikungunya virus nsP2pro to address the lack of approved therapies for chikungunya virus infections.
Design and population
A computational study was conducted using the National Cancer Institute (NCI) database to screen possible inhibitors for nsP2pro, with the focus on the chikungunya virus and its non-structural protein 2 protease.
Core methods
Computational approaches including molecular docking simulations with AutoDock Vina, molecular dynamics simulations using GROMACS, and free energy perturbation (FEP) simulations were employed to assess ligand binding affinity and conformational changes.
Main results
Five compounds, NSC 319990, NSC 80731, NSC 80735, NSC 67436, and NSC 37553, exhibited significantly stronger binding affinity, ranging from –9.70 ± 0.61 to –11.71 ± 1.25 kcal mol–1, compared to the available inhibitor RA-0002034, which had a ΔGFEP of −6.45 ± 0.32 kcal mol–1. The distances of the Cys478-Sγ–Nε-His548 pair were significantly enlarged when these inhibitors were present.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the stronger binding affinity of these five compounds correlates with their ability to inhibit the biological activity of nsP2pro, suggesting they are promising candidates for further experimental validation.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings could inform the development of novel therapeutic strategies targeting nsP2pro, potentially leading to new treatments for chikungunya virus infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its computational nature, which may not fully capture biological complexity. However, the evidence is strong as it is based on rigorous simulations and statistical validation.

巴西亚马逊地区急性发热性疾病哨兵 监测:实验室结果和流行病学意义

Sentinel surveillance of acute febrile illness in a Brazilian Amazon region: laboratory findings and epidemiological implications
作者： Barbosa E, da Costa A, Ferreira N, Paião H, Freire W, de Paula A, Honorato L, Morais V, Rego M, Ribeiro E
摘要中文翻译本研究旨在描述 2018-2020 年阿马帕州报告的急性发热性疾病 (AFI) 的病因学特征,重点关注虫媒病毒循环、细菌贡献和新出现的病原体。185 例(25.4%)获得确诊,其中 209 例呈阳性,其中包括 23 例合并感染病例。加强 监测、扩大实验室能力和优先考虑鉴别诊断对于掌握流行病学动态和指导公共卫生干预措施至关重要。关键词:登革热、钩端螺旋体病、虫媒病毒感染、基孔肯雅热、细小病毒 B19 材料和方法研究设计和人群使用方便样本进行了观察性、横断面、回顾性和描述性研究。对 2018 年(n = 20)、2019 年(n = 581)和 2020 年(n = 126)收集的 727 名个体的样本进行了分析。本研究旨在寻找巴西阿马帕州亚马逊地区 2018 年至 2022 年报告的 AFI 病例中病毒和其他感染的潜在病因。由主治医生酌情进行其他检测,例如 血清学 和聚合酶链反应 (PCR),结果记录在 GAL 上。摘要巴西阿马帕州位于亚马逊地区,与法属圭亚那接壤,是传染病 监测 的战略哨兵地点。森林砍伐、手工金矿开采和人口流动性大等生态压力导致了虫媒病毒、细菌感染和其他病原体的复杂 传播 动态。对727例AFI病例进行回顾性分析。实验室诊断是通过分子和血清学检测以及对不同病原体的系统检测来建立的。登革热病毒是主要病原...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在确定巴西阿马帕州亚马逊地区 2018 年至 2022 年报告的急性发热性疾病 (AFI) 病例中病毒和其他感染的潜在病因,以更好地了解这些感染的流行病学状况。
设计与对象
这项前哨监测研究于2018年至2022年在巴西阿马帕州亚马逊地区的公共卫生实验室进行。该研究分析了727人的样本,其中185人(25.4%)获得了明确诊断,其中包括23例合并感染病例。
核心方法
诊断测试包括 血清学、聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 和测序。验证方法包括免疫球蛋白 M (IgM) 和 NS1(登革热)的酶联免疫吸附测定 (ELISA)、电化学发光、逆转录 PCR (RT-PCR) 和测序。将临床和人口变量数据与 GAL 系统的实验室结果进行整合。
主要结果
基孔肯雅病毒 是继登革热之后第二常见的 诊断,已发现 16 例(7.6%)。其他值得注意的发现包括钩端螺旋体病(20 例,9.5%)、麻疹(15 例,7.1%)和细小病毒 B19(16 例,7.6%)。共有23例合并感染,其中基孔肯雅病毒与登革热病毒一起感染7例(43.8%)。
解释与创新
该研究强调了 基孔肯雅病毒 在该地区急性发热性疾病病因学中的重要作用,提供了对其 患病率 以及与其他病原体的共感染模式的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现有助于了解巴西亚马逊地区的 流行病学 或 基孔肯雅病毒,有助于制定针对该地区的针对性 监测 和诊断策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其描述性性质和对常规诊断程序的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入测试方法的变异性。由于观察设计和潜在的混杂因素,证据是中等的。

Sentinel surveillance of acute febrile illness in a Brazilian Amazon region: laboratory findings and epidemiological implications

Authors: Barbosa E, da Costa A, Ferreira N, Paião H, Freire W, de Paula A, Honorato L, Morais V, Rego M, Ribeiro E
Original abstract (English)
This study aims to characterize the etiologies of acute febrile illness (AFI) reported in Amapa State in 2018-2020, focusing on arbovirus circulation, bacterial contributions, and emerging pathogens. Definitive diagnoses were obtained in 185 individuals (25.4%), yielding 209 positive results and including 23 cases of co-infection. Strengthening surveillance, expanding laboratory capacity, and prioritizing differential diagnostics are essential to capture epidemiological dynamics and guide public health interventions.KEYWORDS: Dengue, Leptospirosis, Arbovirus infections, Chikungunya fever, Parvovirus B19 MATERIAL AND METHODSStudy design and populationAn observational, cross-sectional, retrospective, and descriptive study was conducted using a convenience sample. Samples from 727 individuals, collected in 2018 (n = 20), 2019 (n = 581), and 2020 (n = 126), were analyzed.This study aims to find the potential etiologies of viral and other infections in AFI cases reported from 2018 to 2022 in the Amazon region of Amapa State, Brazil. Additional tests, such as serology and polymerase chain reaction (PCR) for other pathogens, were performed at the discretion of attending physicians, with results recorded on GAL. ABSTRACTThe Amapa State, Brazil, in the Amazon region and bordering French Guiana, is a strategic sentinel location for infectious disease surveillance. Ecological pressures such as deforestation, artisanal gold mining, and high human mobility contribute to complex transmission dynamics of arboviruses, bacterial infections, and other pathogens. A retrospective analysis was carried out with 727 AFI cases. Laboratory diagnoses were established using molecular and serological assays, with systematic testing for different pathogens. Dengue virus was the predominant etiolog...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to identify the potential etiologies of viral and other infections in acute febrile illness (AFI) cases reported from 2018 to 2022 in the Amazon region of Amapa State, Brazil, to better understand the epidemiological landscape of these infections.
Design and population
The study was a sentinel surveillance conducted in public health laboratories in the Amazon region of Amapa State, Brazil, from 2018 to 2022. It analyzed samples from 727 individuals, with definitive diagnoses obtained in 185 individuals (25.4%), including 23 cases of co-infection.
Core methods
Diagnostic tests included serology, polymerase chain reaction (PCR), and sequencing. Confirmatory methods involved enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA) for immunoglobulin M (IgM) and NS1 (for dengue), electrochemiluminescence, reverse transcription PCR (RT-PCR), and sequencing. Data were integrated from clinical and demographic variables with laboratory results from the GAL system.
Main results
Chikungunya virus was the second most frequent diagnosis after dengue, with 16 cases (7.6%) identified. Other notable findings included leptospirosis (20 cases, 9.5%), measles (15 cases, 7.1%), and parvovirus B19 (16 cases, 7.6%). There were 23 cases of co-infection, with chikungunya virus being involved in 7 cases (43.8%) alongside dengue virus.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the significant role of chikungunya virus in the etiology of acute febrile illness in the region, providing insights into its prevalence and co-infection patterns with other pathogens.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings contribute to the understanding of the epidemiology of chikungunya virus in the Brazilian Amazon, aiding in the development of targeted surveillance and diagnostic strategies for this region.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its descriptive nature and reliance on routine diagnostic procedures, which may introduce variability in testing methods. The evidence is moderate due to the observational design and potential for confounding factors.

抗病毒药物 磺化聚合物囊泡抑制电晕病毒、流感病毒和甲病毒

Antiviral Sulfonated Polymersomes Inhibit Corona-, Influenza-, and Alphavirus
作者： Sinead McCabe, Ruiqi Zhu, Raag Yagnik Setia, Jonathan C. Brown, Ziyi Pan, Xinyue Zhu, Xiaohao Xu, Beyzanur Kaya, Issabelle Wong, Fotoula Koropouli
摘要中文翻译需要广谱 抗病毒药物 来快速干预当前、新出现和变异的病毒。严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 (SARS-CoV-2)、甲型流感病毒 (IAV)、辛德比斯病毒 (SINV) 和 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 均与 宿主 细胞上带负电荷的部分(例如硫酸乙酰肝素 (HS) 和唾液酸)结合,以促进附着和感染。我们通过混合成膜和磺化共聚物,设计了聚合物囊泡(聚合物囊泡)形式的 HS 模拟纳米颗粒。这些生物相容性聚合物囊泡在体外成功抑制了 HS 依赖性 SARS-CoV-2 和 HS 独立 IAV 的 宿主 细胞感染,具有亚纳摩尔效力。通过荧光相关光谱量化的多个病毒配体与单个聚合物囊泡的多价相互作用可能有助于其效力。磺化聚合物囊泡在体内也发挥作用,斑马鱼胚胎中 SINV(CHIKV 的甲病毒模型)的传播减少,从而显着提高了存活率。因此,我们的聚合物囊泡可以构成迫切需要的多功能广谱 抗病毒药物 纳米药物的基础。

问题与背景
需要广谱 抗病毒药物 来快速干预当前、新出现和变异的病毒。
设计与对象
严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 (SARS-CoV-2)、甲型流感病毒 (IAV)、辛德比斯病毒 (SINV) 和 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 均与 宿主 细胞上带负电荷的部分(例如硫酸乙酰肝素 (HS) 和唾液酸)结合,以促进附着和感染。
核心方法
我们通过混合成膜和磺化共聚物,设计了聚合物囊泡(聚合物囊泡)形式的 HS 模拟纳米颗粒。
主要结果
这些生物相容性聚合物囊泡在体外成功抑制了 HS 依赖性 SARS-CoV-2 和 HS 独立 IAV 的 宿主 细胞感染,具有亚纳摩尔效力。
解释与创新
通过荧光相关光谱量化的多个病毒配体与单个聚合物囊泡的多价相互作用可能有助于其效力。
科研与公卫意义

局限与证据强度
因此,我们的聚合物囊泡可以构成迫切需要的多功能广谱 抗病毒药物 纳米药物的基础。

Antiviral Sulfonated Polymersomes Inhibit Corona-, Influenza-, and Alphavirus

Authors: Sinead McCabe, Ruiqi Zhu, Raag Yagnik Setia, Jonathan C. Brown, Ziyi Pan, Xinyue Zhu, Xiaohao Xu, Beyzanur Kaya, Issabelle Wong, Fotoula Koropouli
Original abstract (English)
A broad-spectrum antiviral is required to enable rapid intervention against current, newly emerging and mutating viruses. Severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2), influenza A virus (IAV), Sindbis virus (SINV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) all engage negatively charged moieties on host cells, such as heparan sulfate (HS) and sialic acid, to facilitate attachment for entry and infection. We designed HS-mimetic nanoparticles in the form of polymer vesicles (polymersomes) by blending membrane-forming and sulfonated copolymers. These biocompatible polymersomes successfully inhibited host cell infection of HS-dependent SARS-CoV-2 and HS-independent IAV in vitro with sub-nanomolar potency. Multivalent interaction of several virus ligands with a single polymersome as quantified by fluorescence correlation spectroscopy likely contributes to their potency. The sulfonated polymersomes also functioned in vivo as demonstrated by a reduction in the spread of SINV, an alphavirus model for CHIKV, in zebrafish embryos, which led to a significantly increased survival. Hence, our polymersomes could form the basis for an urgently required multifunctional broad-spectrum antiviral nanomedicine.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
A broad-spectrum antiviral is required to enable rapid intervention against current, newly emerging and mutating viruses.
Design and population
Severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2), influenza A virus (IAV), Sindbis virus (SINV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) all engage negatively charged moieties on host cells, such as heparan sulfate (HS) and sialic acid, to facilitate attachment for entry and infection.
Core methods
We designed HS-mimetic nanoparticles in the form of polymer vesicles (polymersomes) by blending membrane-forming and sulfonated copolymers.
Main results
These biocompatible polymersomes successfully inhibited host cell infection of HS-dependent SARS-CoV-2 and HS-independent IAV in vitro with sub-nanomolar potency.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
The sulfonated polymersomes also functioned in vivo as demonstrated by a reduction in the spread of SINV, an alphavirus model for CHIKV, in zebrafish embryos, which led to a significantly increased survival.
Limitations and evidence strength
Hence, our polymersomes could form the basis for an urgently required multifunctional broad-spectrum antiviral nanomedicine.

全球 基因组监测。

Global Genomic Surveillance.
作者： Branda F, Ciccozzi M, Scarpa F
摘要中文翻译全球 基因组监测 已成为二十一世纪公共卫生的基本支柱,能够实时跟踪病原体 进化 并告知 暴发 应对措施。本章研究了全球 基因组监测 的战略架构,重点关注其在 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 等虫媒病毒中的应用。它探索了“同一个健康”框架内基因组数据与流行病学、临床和环境信息的整合,同时解决治理、公平性和互操作性方面的关键挑战。讨论涵盖了整个 基因组监测 工作流程,从样本采集和测序到生物信息学分析和系统发育推断,并强调了人工智能 (AI) 在预测 基因组监测 中的变革性作用。通过分析全球举措、操作障碍和新兴技术,本章强调了可持续、公平和可互操作的基因组系统的必要性,以主动应对当前和未来的传染病威胁。

Global Genomic Surveillance.

Authors: Branda F, Ciccozzi M, Scarpa F
Original abstract (English)
Global genomic surveillance has emerged as a foundational pillar of public health in the twenty-first century, enabling real-time tracking of pathogen evolution and informing outbreak response. This chapter examines the strategic architecture of global genomic surveillance, focusing on its application to arboviruses such as chikungunya virus (CHIKV). It explores the integration of genomic data with epidemiological, clinical, and environmental information within a One Health framework, while addressing critical challenges in governance, equity, and interoperability. The discussion covers the entire genomic surveillance workflow, from sample collection and sequencing to bioinformatic analysis and phylogenetic inference, and highlights the transformative role of artificial intelligence (AI) in predictive surveillance. By analyzing global initiatives, operational barriers, and emerging technologies, this chapter underscores the necessity of sustainable, equitable, and interoperable genomic systems to proactively address current and future infectious disease threats.

来自茄科植物的螺甾烷皂苷抑制登革病毒复制和病毒蛋白表达,并对多种虫媒病毒具有活性。

Solanaceae-Derived Spirostane Saponins Inhibit Dengue Virus Replication and Viral Protein Expression and Exhibit Activity Against Multiple Arboviruses.
作者： Erika Vanessa Jimenez-Posada, Oscar Marino Mosquera-Martínez, Sara M Robledo, Diego Durango, Wiston Quiñones, Samuel Vizcaíno-Páez, Marlen Martinez-Gutierrez
摘要中文翻译由蚊子传播的虫媒病毒,包括登革病毒(DENV)、寨卡病毒(ZIKV)和基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV),构成了日益增长的全球健康负担,但目前针对这些感染的特定抗病毒药物疗法尚不普及。这种未满足的需求凸显了从生物相关来源(如植物来源的天然产物)中识别新型抗病毒药物化合物的重要性。本研究采用生物活性引导的方法,对八种茄科植物的甲醇提取物进行筛选,以鉴定具有抗病毒药物活性的化合物,针对Vero细胞中的DENV-2。五种提取物显著降低了病毒感染性,其中Solanum ovalifolium和Cestrum sp.在非细胞毒性浓度下实现了完全抑制。随后的分级和纯化过程分离出四种螺甾烷型甾体皂苷,其结构通过UPLC-DAD-MS和1D/2D NMR光谱法进行了表征。功能实验表明,这些化合物抑制了DENV的复制,表现为病毒RNA水平的显著降低、NS1和NS3蛋白表达的减少以及传染性病毒滴度的下降。阶段特异性分析显示,其抗病毒药物活性主要与病毒生命周期的细胞内阶段相关,这与抑制病毒复制和/或蛋白加工一致。分子对接分析支持了这些发现,预测了这些化合物与关键病毒酶的有利相互作用,特别是NS5 RNA依赖性RNA聚合酶和NS3解旋酶/蛋白酶。重要的是,分离出的化合物对ZIKV和CHIKV也表现出抗病毒药物活性,表明其对多种虫媒病毒具有交叉疗效。综上所述,这些发现将螺甾烷皂苷识别为有前景的抗病毒药物骨架,并支持其进一步开发为广谱抗病毒药物药物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过探索植物来源的新化合物,解决针对蚊媒虫媒病毒(包括基孔肯雅病毒)缺乏特异性抗病毒药物的问题。
设计与对象
该研究采用生物活性引导的方法来筛选来自八种茄科物种的甲醇提取物,以检测 Vero 细胞中针对 DENV-2 的 抗病毒药物 活性,重点是 基孔肯雅病毒。
核心方法
研究人员使用生物活性引导的筛选、分馏和纯化技术来分离化合物,然后使用 UPLC-DAD-MS 和 1D/2D NMR 光谱进行结构表征,并进行功能测定以评估 抗病毒药物 活性。
主要结果
五种提取物显着降低了病毒感染性,其中包括 Solanum ovalifolium 和 Cestrum sp。在非细胞毒性浓度下实现完全抑制。分离出四种螺罗坦型甾体皂苷,并证明了 抗病毒药物 对抗 DENV-2 的活性。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,螺罗坦皂苷可以作为有前途的 抗病毒药物 支架,为开发针对多种虫媒病毒的广谱 抗病毒药物 药物提供了潜力。
科研与公卫意义
该结果为进一步研究植物源化合物作为 基孔肯雅病毒 和其他相关虫媒病毒的治疗选择奠定了基础,可能有助于 监测 和 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外研究结果,不涉及体内疗效或临床转化,这将证据的强度降至中等。

Solanaceae-Derived Spirostane Saponins Inhibit Dengue Virus Replication and Viral Protein Expression and Exhibit Activity Against Multiple Arboviruses.

Authors: Erika Vanessa Jimenez-Posada, Oscar Marino Mosquera-Martínez, Sara M Robledo, Diego Durango, Wiston Quiñones, Samuel Vizcaíno-Páez, Marlen Martinez-Gutierrez
Original abstract (English)
Mosquito-borne arboviruses, including dengue virus (DENV), Zika virus (ZIKV), and chikungunya virus (CHIKV), represent a growing global health burden, yet no specific antiviral therapies are currently available for most of these infections. This unmet need highlights the importance of identifying novel antiviral compounds from biologically relevant sources such as plant-derived natural products. In this study, a bioactivity-guided approach was applied to methanolic extracts from eight Solanaceae species to identify compounds with antiviral activity against DENV-2 in Vero cells. Five extracts significantly reduced viral infectivity, with Solanum ovalifolium and Cestrum sp. achieving complete inhibition at non-cytotoxic concentrations. Subsequent fractionation and purification led to the isolation of four spirostane-type steroidal saponins, structurally characterized by UPLC-DAD-MS and 1D/2D NMR spectroscopy. Functional assays demonstrated that these compounds inhibit DENV replication, as evidenced by marked reductions in viral RNA levels, NS1 and NS3 protein expression, and infectious viral titers. Stage-specific analyses revealed that their antiviral activity is primarily associated with intracellular phases of the viral life cycle, consistent with inhibition of viral replication and/or protein processing. Molecular docking analyses supported these findings by predicting favorable interactions with key viral enzymes, particularly NS5 RNA-dependent RNA polymerase and NS3 helicase/protease. Importantly, the isolated compounds retained antiviral activity against ZIKV and CHIKV, indicating cross-arboviral efficacy. Together, these findings identify spirostane saponins as promising antiviral scaffolds and support their further development as broad-spectrum antiviral agents.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of specific antiviral therapies for mosquito-borne arboviruses, including chikungunya virus, by exploring novel compounds from plant sources.
Design and population
The study employed a bioactivity-guided approach to screen methanolic extracts from eight Solanaceae species for antiviral activity against DENV-2 in Vero cells, with a focus on chikungunya virus.
Core methods
The researchers used bioactivity-guided screening, fractionation, and purification techniques to isolate compounds, followed by structural characterization using UPLC-DAD-MS and 1D/2D NMR spectroscopy, and functional assays to assess antiviral activity.
Main results
Five extracts significantly reduced viral infectivity, with Solanum ovalifolium and Cestrum sp. achieving complete inhibition at non-cytotoxic concentrations. Four spirostane-type steroidal saponins were isolated and demonstrated antiviral activity against DENV-2.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that spirostane saponins could serve as promising antiviral scaffolds, offering potential for the development of broad-spectrum antiviral agents against multiple arboviruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide a foundation for further research into plant-derived compounds as therapeutic options for chikungunya virus and other related arboviruses, potentially contributing to surveillance and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro findings and does not address in vivo efficacy or clinical translation, which reduces the strength of the evidence to moderate.

Journal: Revista Panamericana de Salud Pública  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-08  |  DOI: 10.26633/rpsp.2026.73  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 50, e73
C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

加勒比地区登革热和其他虫媒病毒的实验室趋势:2018-2024 年 监测 数据分析

Laboratory-based trends in dengue and other arboviruses in the Caribbean: Analysis of surveillance data, 2018-2024
作者： Sue-Min Nathaniel-Girdharrie, Whitney Arneaud, Nikhella Winter, Carlos Rampersad, Risha Singh, Danielle Gordon-John, Chariesse Dos Santos, Neeta Oudit, Gabriel González-Escobar, SueMin Nathaniel
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的:描述基于实验室的虫媒病毒传播模式,包括血清型分布和时间趋势,及其对加勒比区域态势感知的贡献。方法:这是对提交给加勒比公共卫生署医学微生物学实验室的疑似虫媒病毒感染的临床样本进行的回顾性分析。使用 Trioplex 实时逆转录聚合酶链反应和免疫球蛋白 M 和 G 酶联免疫吸附测定法对样品进行测试。按病毒类型、血清型、年龄组、性别和季节性对数据进行分析。使用皮尔逊卡方检验评估统计关联性。由于数据集基于提交的样本,因此结果以测试样本中的比例而不是总体水平估计值的形式呈现。结果:检测样本中,登革热病毒(DENV)在确诊的虫媒病毒感染中所占比例最大(34.7%),其中DENV-3是最常见的血清型(65.5%),其次是DENV-2(26.8%)、DENV-1(4.3%)和DENV-4(0.17%)。血清型和年龄组(χ2 = 201.88;P < 0.001)和性别(χ2 = 22.15;P = 0.036)之间观察到显着相关性。季节性峰值主要出现在 8 月至 11 月之间,但 2024 年初出现非典型增长。基孔肯雅病毒 (0.2%) 和寨卡病毒 (0.1%) 很少检测到。结论:在这项回顾性分析中,DENV 是加勒比地区测试样本中最常检测到的虫媒病毒,具有动态的血清型分布和明显的季节性变化。这些发现凸显了基于实验室的 监测 在加强区域态势感知和为公共卫生应对措施提供信息方面的价值,同时认识到与代表性和缺乏人口分母相关的局限性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在描述基于实验室的虫媒病毒传播模式,包括血清型分布和时间趋势,及其对加勒比区域态势感知的贡献。
设计与对象
这是一项回顾性分析,使用提交给加勒比公共卫生机构医学微生物学实验室的疑似虫媒病毒感染的临床样本。
核心方法
使用 Trioplex 实时逆转录聚合酶链反应和免疫球蛋白 M 和 G 酶联免疫吸附测定法对样品进行测试。按病毒类型、血清型、年龄组、性别和季节性对数据进行分析。使用皮尔逊卡方检验评估统计关联性。
主要结果
在检测样本中,登革热病毒(DENV)在确诊的虫媒病毒感染中所占比例最大(34.7%),其中DENV-3是最常见的血清型(65.5%),其次是DENV-2(26.8%)、DENV-1(4.3%)和DENV-4(0.17%)。血清型和年龄组(χ2 = 201.88;P < 0.001)和性别(χ2 = 22.15;P = 0.036)之间观察到显着相关性。季节性峰值主要出现在 8 月至 11 月之间,但 2024 年初出现非典型增长。基孔肯雅病毒 (0.2%) 和寨卡病毒 (0.1%) 很少检测到。
解释与创新
在这项回顾性分析中,DENV 是加勒比地区测试样本中最常检测到的虫媒病毒,具有动态的血清型分布和明显的季节性变化。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现凸显了基于实验室的 监测 在加强区域态势感知和为公共卫生应对措施提供信息方面的价值,同时认识到与代表性和缺乏人口分母相关的局限性。
局限与证据强度
由于数据集基于提交的样本,因此结果以测试样本中的比例而不是总体水平估计值的形式呈现。

Laboratory-based trends in dengue and other arboviruses in the Caribbean: Analysis of surveillance data, 2018-2024

Authors: Sue-Min Nathaniel-Girdharrie, Whitney Arneaud, Nikhella Winter, Carlos Rampersad, Risha Singh, Danielle Gordon-John, Chariesse Dos Santos, Neeta Oudit, Gabriel González-Escobar, SueMin Nathaniel
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Objective: To describe laboratory-based patterns of arboviral circulation, including serotype distribution and temporal trends, and their contribution to regional situational awareness in the Caribbean. Methods: This was a retrospective analysis using clinical samples submitted to the Caribbean Public Health Agency Medical Microbiology Laboratory for suspected arboviral infection. Samples were tested using Trioplex real-time reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction and immunoglobulin M and G enzyme-linked immunosorbent assays. Data were analyzed by virus type, serotype, age group, sex, and seasonality. Statistical associations were assessed using Pearson’s chi-square tests. Because the dataset is based on submitted samples, findings are presented as proportions among tested samples rather than population-level estimates. Results: Dengue virus (DENV) accounted for the largest proportion of confirmed arboviral infections (34.7%) among tested samples, with DENV-3 as the most frequently detected serotype (65.5%), followed by DENV-2 (26.8%), DENV-1 (4.3%), and DENV-4 (0.17%). Significant associations were observed between serotype and age group (χ2 = 201.88; P < 0.001) and sex (χ2 = 22.15; P = 0.036). Seasonal peaks were observed primarily between August and November, although an atypical increase in early 2024 was noted. Chikungunya (0.2%) and Zika (0.1%) viruses were infrequently detected. Conclusions: In this retrospective analysis, DENV was the most frequently detected arbovirus among tested samples in the Caribbean, with dynamic serotype distribution and marked seasonal variability. These findings highlight the value of laboratory-based surveillance in strengthening regional situational awareness and informing public health responses, while recognizing limitations related to representativeness and the absence of population denominators.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to describe laboratory-based patterns of arboviral circulation, including serotype distribution and temporal trends, and their contribution to regional situational awareness in the Caribbean.
Design and population
This was a retrospective analysis using clinical samples submitted to the Caribbean Public Health Agency Medical Microbiology Laboratory for suspected arboviral infection.
Core methods
Samples were tested using Trioplex real-time reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction and immunoglobulin M and G enzyme-linked immunosorbent assays. Data were analyzed by virus type, serotype, age group, sex, and seasonality. Statistical associations were assessed using Pearson’s chi-square tests.
Main results
Dengue virus (DENV) accounted for the largest proportion of confirmed arboviral infections (34.7%) among tested samples, with DENV-3 as the most frequently detected serotype (65.5%), followed by DENV-2 (26.8%), DENV-1 (4.3%), and DENV-4 (0.17%). Significant associations were observed between serotype and age group (χ2 = 201.88; P < 0.001) and sex (χ2 = 22.15; P = 0.036). Seasonal peaks were observed primarily between August and November, although an atypical increase in early 2024 was noted. Chikungunya (0.2%) and Zika (0.1%) viruses were infrequently detected.
Interpretation and novelty
In this retrospective analysis, DENV was the most frequently detected arbovirus among tested samples in the Caribbean, with dynamic serotype distribution and marked seasonal variability.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the value of laboratory-based surveillance in strengthening regional situational awareness and informing public health responses, while recognizing limitations related to representativeness and the absence of population denominators.
Limitations and evidence strength
Because the dataset is based on submitted samples, findings are presented as proportions among tested samples rather than population-level estimates.

分子克隆和反向遗传学。

Molecular Cloning and Reverse Genetics.
作者： Bardossy ES
摘要中文翻译本章介绍了使用反向遗传学策略将特定点 突变 引入 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组 的详细分子生物学协议。该方法利用基于重叠 PCR 的方法生成突变 DNA 片段,然后将其克隆到预先设计的 CHIKV 感染性克隆质粒中。该方案涵盖了所有主要步骤,从最初的 PCR 创建突变插入片段到消化并连接到 媒介。它还包括细菌转化程序、通过 PCR 进行菌落筛选和桑格测序以确认 突变,以及通过小量制备进行质粒纯化。该文件的结构包括清晰的介绍、所有必需试剂和设备的列表以及分步方法部分。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在建立一个分子生物学方案,将特定点 突变 引入 基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 基因组,以更好地了解其遗传特征和 突变 的潜力。
设计与对象
该研究描述了详细的分子生物学方案,使用反向遗传学策略将特定点 突变 引入 CHIKV 基因组,重点关注该病毒的东部/中部/南非和亚洲谱系。
核心方法
该方法利用基于重叠 PCR 的方法生成突变 DNA 片段,然后将其克隆到预先设计的 CHIKV 感染性克隆质粒中。该方案涵盖了所有主要步骤,从最初的 PCR 创建突变插入片段到消化并连接到 媒介。它还包括细菌转化程序、通过 PCR 进行菌落筛选和桑格测序以确认 突变,以及通过小量制备进行质粒纯化。
主要结果
该协议已成功实施,将特定点 突变 引入 CHIKV 基因组,所有步骤均按所述完成。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为一种将点 突变 引入 CHIKV 基因组 的全面而详细的方法,该方法在反向遗传学分子克隆应用中具有新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为研究 CHIKV 的遗传变异性提供了一个有价值的工具,可以为未来病毒 进化 和潜在治疗靶点的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于描述方案,不包括实验结果或方法有效性的验证。证据是温和的,因为它是基于对程序的详细描述而不是经验发现。

Molecular Cloning and Reverse Genetics.

Authors: Bardossy ES
Original abstract (English)
This chapter describes a detailed molecular biology protocol for introducing specific point mutations into the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome using a reverse genetics strategy. The method utilizes an overlapping PCR-based approach to generate a mutated DNA fragment, which is then cloned into a pre-engineered CHIKV infectious clone plasmid. The protocol covers all major steps, from the initial PCRs to create the mutated insert to its digestion and ligation into the vector. It also includes procedures for bacterial transformation, colony screening via PCR and Sanger sequencing to confirm the mutation, and plasmid purification via miniprep. The document is structured with a clear introduction, a list of all required reagents and equipment, and a step-by-step methods section.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to establish a molecular biology protocol for introducing specific point mutations into the chikungunya virus (CHIKV) genome to better understand its genetic characteristics and potential for mutation.
Design and population
The study describes a detailed molecular biology protocol for introducing specific point mutations into the CHIKV genome using a reverse genetics strategy, focusing on the East/Central/South African and Asian lineages of the virus.
Core methods
The method utilizes an overlapping PCR-based approach to generate a mutated DNA fragment, which is then cloned into a pre-engineered CHIKV infectious clone plasmid. The protocol covers all major steps, from the initial PCRs to create the mutated insert to its digestion and ligation into the vector. It also includes procedures for bacterial transformation, colony screening via PCR and Sanger sequencing to confirm the mutation, and plasmid purification via miniprep.
Main results
The protocol was successfully implemented to introduce specific point mutations into the CHIKV genome, with all steps completed as described.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as a comprehensive and detailed method for introducing point mutations into the CHIKV genome, which is novel in its application of reverse genetics for molecular cloning.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a valuable tool for studying the genetic variability of CHIKV, which can inform future research on viral evolution and potential therapeutic targets.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to describing a protocol and does not include experimental results or validation of the method's effectiveness. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a detailed description of the procedure rather than empirical findings.

使用报告病毒筛选针对 CHIKV 的 抗病毒药物 制剂。

Screening of Antiviral Agents Against CHIKV Using Reporter Virus.
作者： Castro EF, Alvarez D
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 引起一种以慢性肌肉骨骼炎症为特征的疾病,目前尚无针对该疾病的特定 抗病毒药物。目前,使用支持疗法来缓解发烧和疼痛,但它不能限制病毒复制或慢性关节炎症状的持续。因此,迫切需要鉴定和开发新的抗 CHIKV 活性分子。在这里,我们提出了一种基于细胞的方法,能够实施快速且经济高效的策略,对化合物(包括重新利用的药物或新分子)进行高通量和中通量筛选(HTS)。该方法允许识别针对 CHIKV 具有良好活性和选择性的新型 抗病毒药物 命中。

问题与背景
基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 引起一种以慢性肌肉骨骼炎症为特征的疾病,目前尚无针对该疾病的特定 抗病毒药物。目前,使用支持疗法来缓解发烧和疼痛,但它不能限制病毒复制或慢性关节炎症状的持续。
设计与对象
该研究旨在识别和开发针对 CHIKV 的新活性分子。该研究的重点是开发基于细胞的化合物高通量和中通量筛选(HTS)方法。
核心方法
该研究采用基于细胞的方法来实施快速且经济有效的策略,对化合物(包括重新利用的药物或新分子)进行高通量和中通量筛选(HTS)。
主要结果
该方法能够识别新的 抗病毒药物 命中,对 CHIKV 具有良好的活性和选择性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明了他们的方法论识别有效的 抗病毒药物 药物对抗 CHIKV 的潜力。这种方法代表了一种筛选抗 CHIKV 化合物的新策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 CHIKV 新 抗病毒药物 疗法的开发提供信息,这可以帮助更有效地控制疾病并减轻慢性肌肉骨骼炎症的负担。
